# Corporate fleets (pe_fleet_events.txt): fleets of a corporation, owned by our Private
# Holdings (with one of their leaders) and leased to us (set_controller). Upkeep for us: the
# 10% the engine cannot take off (MIN_ECONOMIC_MODIFIER_MULT), as a leasing fee; no naval
# capacity. Each kind keeps its own variables (prefix), so offers of one quarter do not mix:
#   pe_wl_  war lease (pe.282 / 283): at war, five years, then buy or return
#   pe_cl_  clearing (pe.284 / 285): space creatures next to our border, loot rights
#   pe_cv_  convoy protection (pe.287 / 288): pirates within our borders
#   pe_ch_  exploration charter (pe.286): a science ship for five years, mining rights
#   pe_sv_  salvage (pe.289); pe_sw_ corporate Dyson swarm (pe.292); pe_af_ corporate arc
#           furnace (pe.293); pe_df_ planetary defence (pe.294 / 295, monthly from pe.100)
# Debug (pe.277, flag pe_fl_debug): offers only, running contracts are not advanced.
# Call depths: pe_corp_fleets, pe_corp_charter, pe_corp_convoy, pe_corp_salvage,
# pe_corp_swarm, pe_corp_furnace 3 (from pe_corp_step) -> the start and exec effects,
# pe_select_mid, pe_orbital_value, pe_corp_msg 4.

# Country scope, quarterly: war lease and clearing.
pe_corp_fleets = {
	# --- War lease: running ---
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_lease_war_q value > 0 } NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_fl_debug } }
		change_variable = { which = pe_lease_war_q value = -1 }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { any_controlled_fleet = { has_fleet_flag = pe_lease_war } } }
			set_variable = { which = pe_lease_war_q value = 0 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_lease_war_q value <= 0 } }
			set_variable = { which = pe_fl_price value = pe_wl_ships }
			multiply_variable = { which = pe_fl_price value = pe_wl_cprice }
			if = {
				limit = { is_ai = yes }
				if = {
					limit = { has_resource = { type = alloys amount >= 500 } }
					pe_corp_fleet_buy = yes
				}
				else = { every_controlled_fleet = { limit = { has_fleet_flag = pe_lease_war } if = { limit = { exists = leader } kill_leader = { show_notification = no fire = yes } } delete_fleet = this } }
			}
			else = { country_event = { id = pe.283 } }
		}
	}
	# --- War lease: an offer (at war, an industry corporation with 600 cash, otherwise any
	# with 1200, 30% a quarter, then five years' pause) ---
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = pe_lease_war_q value > 0 } }
			OR = { is_at_war = yes has_country_flag = pe_fl_debug }
			exists = event_target:pe_hold
			NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_lease_war_cd }
		}
		set_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_wl_best value = 599 }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c1_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c1_grp value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c1_cash value > pe_wl_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 1 } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_best value = pe_c1_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c2_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c2_grp value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c2_cash value > pe_wl_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 2 } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_best value = pe_c2_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c3_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c3_grp value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c3_cash value > pe_wl_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 3 } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_best value = pe_c3_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c4_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c4_grp value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c4_cash value > pe_wl_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 4 } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_best value = pe_c4_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c5_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c5_grp value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c5_cash value > pe_wl_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 5 } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_best value = pe_c5_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c6_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c6_grp value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c6_cash value > pe_wl_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 6 } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_best value = pe_c6_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c7_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c7_grp value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c7_cash value > pe_wl_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 7 } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_best value = pe_c7_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c8_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c8_grp value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c8_cash value > pe_wl_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 8 } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_best value = pe_c8_cash } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 0 } }
			set_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 0 }
			set_variable = { which = pe_wl_best value = 1199 }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c1_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c1_cash value > pe_wl_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 1 } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_best value = pe_c1_cash } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c2_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c2_cash value > pe_wl_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 2 } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_best value = pe_c2_cash } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c3_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c3_cash value > pe_wl_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 3 } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_best value = pe_c3_cash } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c4_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c4_cash value > pe_wl_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 4 } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_best value = pe_c4_cash } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c5_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c5_cash value > pe_wl_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 5 } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_best value = pe_c5_cash } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c6_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c6_cash value > pe_wl_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 6 } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_best value = pe_c6_cash } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c7_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c7_cash value > pe_wl_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 7 } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_best value = pe_c7_cash } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c8_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c8_cash value > pe_wl_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 8 } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_best value = pe_c8_cash } }
		}
		# Our defence contractor (pe_dc_k) comes first.
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_dc_k value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c1_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c1_cash value >= 600 } } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 1 } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_best value = pe_c1_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_dc_k value = 2 } check_variable = { which = pe_c2_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c2_cash value >= 600 } } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 2 } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_best value = pe_c2_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_dc_k value = 3 } check_variable = { which = pe_c3_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c3_cash value >= 600 } } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 3 } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_best value = pe_c3_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_dc_k value = 4 } check_variable = { which = pe_c4_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c4_cash value >= 600 } } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 4 } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_best value = pe_c4_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_dc_k value = 5 } check_variable = { which = pe_c5_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c5_cash value >= 600 } } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 5 } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_best value = pe_c5_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_dc_k value = 6 } check_variable = { which = pe_c6_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c6_cash value >= 600 } } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 6 } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_best value = pe_c6_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_dc_k value = 7 } check_variable = { which = pe_c7_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c7_cash value >= 600 } } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 7 } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_best value = pe_c7_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_dc_k value = 8 } check_variable = { which = pe_c8_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c8_cash value >= 600 } } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 8 } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_best value = pe_c8_cash } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value > 0 } }
			random_list = {
				30 = {
					set_timed_country_flag = { flag = pe_lease_war_cd days = 1800 }
					set_variable = { which = pe_wl_budget value = pe_wl_best }
					multiply_variable = { which = pe_wl_budget value = 0.5 }
					export_trigger_value_to_variable = { trigger = fleet_power variable = pe_wl_need }
					multiply_variable = { which = pe_wl_need value = 0.25 }
					if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_wl_need value < 500 } } set_variable = { which = pe_wl_need value = 500 } }
					capital_scope = { save_event_target_as = pe_wl_loc }
					if = {
						limit = { is_ai = yes }
						random = { chance = 80 pe_corp_fleet_war_start = yes }
					}
					else = { country_event = { id = pe.282 } }
				}
				70 = { modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { which = pe_fl_force value = 1 } } }
			}
		}
	}
	# --- Clearing: running ---
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_lease_clear_q value > 0 } NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_fl_debug } }
		change_variable = { which = pe_lease_clear_q value = -1 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_cl_done value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { any_controlled_fleet = { has_fleet_flag = pe_lease_clear } } }
			set_variable = { which = pe_cl_done value = 2 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { any_system = { has_star_flag = pe_clear@root NOT = { pe_monster_system = yes } } }
			set_variable = { which = pe_cl_done value = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_lease_clear_q value <= 0 } }
			set_variable = { which = pe_cl_done value = 2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cl_done value > 0 } }
			random_system = {
				limit = { has_star_flag = pe_clear@root }
				save_event_target_as = pe_cl_sys
				remove_star_flag = pe_clear@root
			}
			every_controlled_fleet = { limit = { has_fleet_flag = pe_lease_clear } if = { limit = { exists = leader } kill_leader = { show_notification = no fire = yes } } delete_fleet = this }
			set_variable = { which = pe_lease_clear_q value = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cl_done value = 1 } }
				# The loot: a third of the creatures' power for the corporation in cash (plus its
				# ships back), a twentieth for us as society research (at least 25).
				set_variable = { which = pe_cl_loot value = pe_cl_power }
				multiply_variable = { which = pe_cl_loot value = 0.35 }
				change_variable = { which = pe_cl_loot value = pe_cl_spent }
				round_variable = pe_cl_loot
				set_variable = { which = pe_cl_res value = pe_cl_power }
				multiply_variable = { which = pe_cl_res value = 0.05 }
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cl_res value < 25 } } set_variable = { which = pe_cl_res value = 25 } }
				round_variable = pe_cl_res
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cl_run value = 1 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c1_cash value = pe_cl_loot } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cl_run value = 2 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c2_cash value = pe_cl_loot } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cl_run value = 3 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c3_cash value = pe_cl_loot } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cl_run value = 4 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c4_cash value = pe_cl_loot } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cl_run value = 5 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c5_cash value = pe_cl_loot } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cl_run value = 6 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c6_cash value = pe_cl_loot } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cl_run value = 7 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c7_cash value = pe_cl_loot } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cl_run value = 8 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c8_cash value = pe_cl_loot } }
				add_resource = { society_research = 1 mult = pe_cl_res }
			}
			if = { limit = { is_ai = no } country_event = { id = pe.285 } }
		}
	}
	# --- Clearing: an offer (creatures next to our border, a corporation with 600 cash, 25%,
	# then four years' pause) ---
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = pe_lease_clear_q value > 0 } }
			exists = event_target:pe_hold
			NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_lease_clear_cd }
			any_system_within_border = { any_neighbor_system = { NOT = { exists = owner } pe_monster_system = yes } }
		}
		set_variable = { which = pe_cl_k value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_cl_best value = 599 }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c1_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c1_cash value > pe_cl_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_cl_k value = 1 } set_variable = { which = pe_cl_best value = pe_c1_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c2_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c2_cash value > pe_cl_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_cl_k value = 2 } set_variable = { which = pe_cl_best value = pe_c2_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c3_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c3_cash value > pe_cl_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_cl_k value = 3 } set_variable = { which = pe_cl_best value = pe_c3_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c4_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c4_cash value > pe_cl_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_cl_k value = 4 } set_variable = { which = pe_cl_best value = pe_c4_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c5_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c5_cash value > pe_cl_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_cl_k value = 5 } set_variable = { which = pe_cl_best value = pe_c5_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c6_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c6_cash value > pe_cl_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_cl_k value = 6 } set_variable = { which = pe_cl_best value = pe_c6_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c7_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c7_cash value > pe_cl_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_cl_k value = 7 } set_variable = { which = pe_cl_best value = pe_c7_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c8_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c8_cash value > pe_cl_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_cl_k value = 8 } set_variable = { which = pe_cl_best value = pe_c8_cash } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cl_k value > 0 } }
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					set_timed_country_flag = { flag = pe_lease_clear_cd days = 1440 }
					random_system_within_border = {
						limit = { any_neighbor_system = { NOT = { exists = owner } pe_monster_system = yes } }
						save_event_target_as = pe_cl_loc
						random_neighbor_system = {
							limit = { NOT = { exists = owner } pe_monster_system = yes }
							save_event_target_as = pe_cl_sys
						}
					}
					set_variable = { which = pe_cl_power value = 0 }
					event_target:pe_cl_sys = {
						every_fleet_in_system = {
							limit = { owner = { OR = { is_amoeba_country_type = yes is_crystal_country_type = yes is_drone_country_type = yes is_voidworms_hostile_country_type = yes } } }
							export_trigger_value_to_variable = { trigger = fleet_power variable = pe_tmp }
							root = { change_variable = { which = pe_cl_power value = prev.pe_tmp } }
						}
					}
					set_variable = { which = pe_cl_need value = pe_cl_power }
					multiply_variable = { which = pe_cl_need value = 1.5 }
					set_variable = { which = pe_cl_budget value = pe_cl_best }
					multiply_variable = { which = pe_cl_budget value = 0.8 }
					if = {
						limit = { is_ai = yes }
						random = { chance = 80 pe_corp_fleet_clear_start = yes }
					}
					else = { country_event = { id = pe.284 } }
				}
				75 = { modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { which = pe_fl_force value = 1 } } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# Country scope: start the war lease (five years).
pe_corp_fleet_war_start = {
	set_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 1 }
	if = { limit = { has_technology = tech_destroyers } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 2 } }
	if = { limit = { has_technology = tech_cruisers } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 3 } }
	if = { limit = { has_technology = tech_battleships } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 4 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_ccost value = 150 } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_cprice value = 40 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_ccost value = 300 } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_cprice value = 80 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_ccost value = 600 } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_cprice value = 160 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 4 } } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_ccost value = 1200 } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_cprice value = 320 } }
	set_variable = { which = pe_wl_spent value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = pe_wl_ships value = 0 }
	create_fleet = {
		name = pe_corp_fleet_name
		settings = { uses_naval_capacity = no }
		effect = {
			set_owner = root
			set_variable = { which = pe_fp value = 0 }
			while = {
				count = 12
				limit = {
					check_variable = { which = pe_fp value < root.pe_wl_need }
					root = { check_variable = { which = pe_wl_spent value < pe_wl_budget } }
				}
				if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 1 } } } create_ship = { name = random random_existing_design = corvette } }
				else_if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 2 } } } create_ship = { name = random random_existing_design = destroyer } }
				else_if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 3 } } } create_ship = { name = random random_existing_design = cruiser } }
				else_if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 4 } } } create_ship = { name = random random_existing_design = battleship } }
				root = {
					change_variable = { which = pe_wl_spent value = pe_fl_ccost }
					change_variable = { which = pe_wl_ships value = 1 }
				}
				export_trigger_value_to_variable = { trigger = fleet_power variable = pe_fp }
			}
			set_location = event_target:pe_wl_loc
			set_fleet_flag = pe_leased
			set_fleet_flag = pe_lease_war
		}
	}
	# The leader is the holdings' own; assigned while they own and control the fleet, then leased.
	event_target:pe_hold = {
		create_leader = { class = commander species = root name = random skill = 2 }
		last_created_leader = { save_event_target_as = pe_fl_leader }
	}
	last_created_fleet = {
		set_owner = event_target:pe_hold
		assign_leader = event_target:pe_fl_leader
		set_controller = root
		add_modifier = { modifier = pe_leased_fleet days = -1 }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { exists = leader } } log = "private_economy: corporate fleet without a leader (assign_leader failed)" }
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 1 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c1_cash value = pe_wl_spent } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 2 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c2_cash value = pe_wl_spent } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 3 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c3_cash value = pe_wl_spent } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 4 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c4_cash value = pe_wl_spent } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 5 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c5_cash value = pe_wl_spent } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 6 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c6_cash value = pe_wl_spent } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 7 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c7_cash value = pe_wl_spent } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_wl_k value = 8 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c8_cash value = pe_wl_spent } }
	set_variable = { which = pe_lease_war_q value = 20 }
	set_variable = { which = pe_wl_cprice value = pe_fl_cprice }
}

# Country scope: start the clearing contract (three years at most).
pe_corp_fleet_clear_start = {
	set_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 1 }
	if = { limit = { has_technology = tech_destroyers } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 2 } }
	if = { limit = { has_technology = tech_cruisers } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 3 } }
	if = { limit = { has_technology = tech_battleships } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 4 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_ccost value = 150 } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_cprice value = 40 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_ccost value = 300 } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_cprice value = 80 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_ccost value = 600 } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_cprice value = 160 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 4 } } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_ccost value = 1200 } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_cprice value = 320 } }
	set_variable = { which = pe_cl_spent value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = pe_cl_ships value = 0 }
	create_fleet = {
		name = pe_corp_fleet_name
		settings = { uses_naval_capacity = no }
		effect = {
			set_owner = root
			set_variable = { which = pe_fp value = 0 }
			while = {
				count = 12
				limit = {
					check_variable = { which = pe_fp value < root.pe_cl_need }
					root = { check_variable = { which = pe_cl_spent value < pe_cl_budget } }
				}
				if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 1 } } } create_ship = { name = random random_existing_design = corvette } }
				else_if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 2 } } } create_ship = { name = random random_existing_design = destroyer } }
				else_if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 3 } } } create_ship = { name = random random_existing_design = cruiser } }
				else_if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 4 } } } create_ship = { name = random random_existing_design = battleship } }
				root = {
					change_variable = { which = pe_cl_spent value = pe_fl_ccost }
					change_variable = { which = pe_cl_ships value = 1 }
				}
				export_trigger_value_to_variable = { trigger = fleet_power variable = pe_fp }
			}
			# An AI empire would not send it on its own: there it starts right at the monsters.
			if = {
				limit = { root = { is_ai = yes } }
				set_location = event_target:pe_cl_sys
				set_fleet_stance = aggressive
			}
			else = { set_location = event_target:pe_cl_loc }
			set_fleet_flag = pe_leased
			set_fleet_flag = pe_lease_clear
		}
	}
	# The leader is the holdings' own; assigned while they own and control the fleet, then leased.
	event_target:pe_hold = {
		create_leader = { class = commander species = root name = random skill = 2 }
		last_created_leader = { save_event_target_as = pe_fl_leader }
	}
	last_created_fleet = {
		set_owner = event_target:pe_hold
		assign_leader = event_target:pe_fl_leader
		set_controller = root
		add_modifier = { modifier = pe_leased_fleet days = -1 }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { exists = leader } } log = "private_economy: corporate fleet without a leader (assign_leader failed)" }
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cl_k value = 1 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c1_cash value = pe_cl_spent } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cl_k value = 2 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c2_cash value = pe_cl_spent } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cl_k value = 3 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c3_cash value = pe_cl_spent } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cl_k value = 4 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c4_cash value = pe_cl_spent } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cl_k value = 5 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c5_cash value = pe_cl_spent } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cl_k value = 6 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c6_cash value = pe_cl_spent } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cl_k value = 7 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c7_cash value = pe_cl_spent } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cl_k value = 8 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c8_cash value = pe_cl_spent } }
	set_variable = { which = pe_lease_clear_q value = 12 }
	set_variable = { which = pe_cl_run value = pe_cl_k }
	event_target:pe_cl_sys = { set_star_flag = pe_clear@root }
}

# Country scope: buy the war fleet (pe_fl_price alloys); it becomes ours, its commander leaves
# (we appoint our own).
pe_corp_fleet_buy = {
	add_resource = { alloys = -1 mult = pe_fl_price }
	every_controlled_fleet = {
		limit = { has_fleet_flag = pe_lease_war }
		if = { limit = { exists = leader } kill_leader = { show_notification = no fire = yes } }
		set_owner = root
		remove_modifier = pe_leased_fleet
		remove_fleet_flag = pe_lease_war
		remove_fleet_flag = pe_leased
	}
	set_variable = { which = pe_lease_war_q value = 0 }
}

# --- Exploration charter (pe.286) ----------------------------------------------------------
# Unsurveyed planets next to our borders: a corporation with 400 cash leases us a science ship
# with one of its scientists for eight years (it pays 300). In return it gets the mining rights
# it finds: when the charter ends, its station pause is over and it may take one station beyond
# the share limit (pe_c<slot>_right). 20% a quarter; after an offer five years' pause, after
# the end of a charter two years; the same corporation offers again after ten years at the
# earliest (pe_charter_cd_<slot>).
pe_corp_charter = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_charter_q value > 0 } NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_fl_debug } }
		change_variable = { which = pe_charter_q value = -1 }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { any_controlled_fleet = { has_fleet_flag = pe_lease_charter } } }
			set_variable = { which = pe_charter_q value = 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_charter_q value <= 0 } }
			every_controlled_fleet = { limit = { has_fleet_flag = pe_lease_charter } if = { limit = { exists = leader } kill_leader = { show_notification = no fire = yes } } delete_fleet = this }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_ch_run value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_c1_right value = 1 } set_variable = { which = pe_c1_scd value = 0 } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c1_name } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_ch_run value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_c2_right value = 1 } set_variable = { which = pe_c2_scd value = 0 } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c2_name } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_ch_run value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_c3_right value = 1 } set_variable = { which = pe_c3_scd value = 0 } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c3_name } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_ch_run value = 4 } } set_variable = { which = pe_c4_right value = 1 } set_variable = { which = pe_c4_scd value = 0 } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c4_name } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_ch_run value = 5 } } set_variable = { which = pe_c5_right value = 1 } set_variable = { which = pe_c5_scd value = 0 } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c5_name } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_ch_run value = 6 } } set_variable = { which = pe_c6_right value = 1 } set_variable = { which = pe_c6_scd value = 0 } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c6_name } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_ch_run value = 7 } } set_variable = { which = pe_c7_right value = 1 } set_variable = { which = pe_c7_scd value = 0 } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c7_name } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_ch_run value = 8 } } set_variable = { which = pe_c8_right value = 1 } set_variable = { which = pe_c8_scd value = 0 } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c8_name } }
			set_timed_country_flag = { flag = pe_charter_cd days = 720 }
			capital_scope = { save_event_target_as = pe_msg_planet }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_ch_run value > 0 } }
				pe_corp_msg = { KEY = pe_msg_charter_end }
			}
			else = {
				create_message = { type = pe_private_economy localization = pe_msg_charter_end_anon days = 30 target = event_target:pe_msg_planet variable = { type = name localization = PLANET scope = event_target:pe_msg_planet } }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = pe_charter_q value > 0 } }
			exists = event_target:pe_hold
			NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_charter_cd }
			any_system_within_border = { any_neighbor_system = { any_system_planet = { is_surveyed = { who = root status = no } } } }
		}
		set_variable = { which = pe_ch_k value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_ch_best value = 399 }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c1_on value = 1 } NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_charter_cd_1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c1_cash value > pe_ch_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_ch_k value = 1 } set_variable = { which = pe_ch_best value = pe_c1_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c2_on value = 1 } NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_charter_cd_2 } check_variable = { which = pe_c2_cash value > pe_ch_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_ch_k value = 2 } set_variable = { which = pe_ch_best value = pe_c2_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c3_on value = 1 } NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_charter_cd_3 } check_variable = { which = pe_c3_cash value > pe_ch_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_ch_k value = 3 } set_variable = { which = pe_ch_best value = pe_c3_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c4_on value = 1 } NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_charter_cd_4 } check_variable = { which = pe_c4_cash value > pe_ch_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_ch_k value = 4 } set_variable = { which = pe_ch_best value = pe_c4_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c5_on value = 1 } NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_charter_cd_5 } check_variable = { which = pe_c5_cash value > pe_ch_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_ch_k value = 5 } set_variable = { which = pe_ch_best value = pe_c5_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c6_on value = 1 } NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_charter_cd_6 } check_variable = { which = pe_c6_cash value > pe_ch_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_ch_k value = 6 } set_variable = { which = pe_ch_best value = pe_c6_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c7_on value = 1 } NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_charter_cd_7 } check_variable = { which = pe_c7_cash value > pe_ch_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_ch_k value = 7 } set_variable = { which = pe_ch_best value = pe_c7_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c8_on value = 1 } NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_charter_cd_8 } check_variable = { which = pe_c8_cash value > pe_ch_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_ch_k value = 8 } set_variable = { which = pe_ch_best value = pe_c8_cash } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_ch_k value > 0 } }
			random_list = {
				20 = {
					set_timed_country_flag = { flag = pe_charter_cd days = 1800 }
					if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_ch_k value = 1 } } set_timed_country_flag = { flag = pe_charter_cd_1 days = 3600 } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_ch_k value = 2 } } set_timed_country_flag = { flag = pe_charter_cd_2 days = 3600 } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_ch_k value = 3 } } set_timed_country_flag = { flag = pe_charter_cd_3 days = 3600 } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_ch_k value = 4 } } set_timed_country_flag = { flag = pe_charter_cd_4 days = 3600 } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_ch_k value = 5 } } set_timed_country_flag = { flag = pe_charter_cd_5 days = 3600 } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_ch_k value = 6 } } set_timed_country_flag = { flag = pe_charter_cd_6 days = 3600 } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_ch_k value = 7 } } set_timed_country_flag = { flag = pe_charter_cd_7 days = 3600 } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_ch_k value = 8 } } set_timed_country_flag = { flag = pe_charter_cd_8 days = 3600 } }
					if = {
						limit = { is_ai = yes }
						random = { chance = 70 pe_corp_charter_start = yes }
					}
					else = { country_event = { id = pe.286 } }
				}
				80 = { modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { which = pe_fl_force value = 1 } } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# Country scope: start the charter (one science ship of our design, a scientist of the holdings).
pe_corp_charter_start = {
	set_variable = { which = pe_charter_q value = 32 }
	set_variable = { which = pe_ch_run value = pe_ch_k }
	create_fleet = {
		name = pe_charter_fleet_name
		settings = { uses_naval_capacity = no }
		effect = {
			set_owner = root
			create_ship = { name = random random_existing_design = science }
			set_location = root.capital_scope
			set_fleet_flag = pe_leased
			set_fleet_flag = pe_lease_charter
		}
	}
	# The leader is the holdings' own; assigned while they own and control the fleet, then leased.
	event_target:pe_hold = {
		create_leader = { class = scientist species = root name = random skill = 2 }
		last_created_leader = { save_event_target_as = pe_fl_leader }
	}
	last_created_fleet = {
		set_owner = event_target:pe_hold
		assign_leader = event_target:pe_fl_leader
		set_controller = root
		add_modifier = { modifier = pe_leased_fleet days = -1 }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { exists = leader } } log = "private_economy: corporate fleet without a leader (assign_leader failed)" }
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_ch_k value = 1 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c1_cash value = -300 } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_ch_k value = 2 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c2_cash value = -300 } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_ch_k value = 3 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c3_cash value = -300 } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_ch_k value = 4 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c4_cash value = -300 } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_ch_k value = 5 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c5_cash value = -300 } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_ch_k value = 6 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c6_cash value = -300 } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_ch_k value = 7 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c7_cash value = -300 } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_ch_k value = 8 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c8_cash value = -300 } }
}

# --- Convoy protection (pe.287 / pe.288) ------------------------------------------------------
# Pirates or marauder raiders in a system within our borders: a corporation with 600 cash lends
# a patrol of 1.5 times their strength (80% of its cash at most), three years at most. Once the
# system is free the patrol returns; the corporation keeps the loot (a third of their power).
# 35% a quarter, then three years' pause.
pe_corp_convoy = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_convoy_q value > 0 } NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_fl_debug } }
		change_variable = { which = pe_convoy_q value = -1 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_cv_done value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { any_controlled_fleet = { has_fleet_flag = pe_lease_convoy } } }
			set_variable = { which = pe_cv_done value = 2 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { any_system = { has_star_flag = pe_convoy@root NOT = { pe_pirate_system = yes } } }
			set_variable = { which = pe_cv_done value = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_convoy_q value <= 0 } }
			set_variable = { which = pe_cv_done value = 2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cv_done value > 0 } }
			random_system = {
				limit = { has_star_flag = pe_convoy@root }
				save_event_target_as = pe_cv_sys
				remove_star_flag = pe_convoy@root
			}
			every_controlled_fleet = { limit = { has_fleet_flag = pe_lease_convoy } if = { limit = { exists = leader } kill_leader = { show_notification = no fire = yes } } delete_fleet = this }
			set_variable = { which = pe_convoy_q value = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cv_done value = 1 } }
				set_variable = { which = pe_cv_loot value = pe_cv_power }
				multiply_variable = { which = pe_cv_loot value = 0.35 }
				change_variable = { which = pe_cv_loot value = pe_cv_spent }
				round_variable = pe_cv_loot
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cv_run value = 1 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c1_cash value = pe_cv_loot } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cv_run value = 2 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c2_cash value = pe_cv_loot } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cv_run value = 3 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c3_cash value = pe_cv_loot } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cv_run value = 4 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c4_cash value = pe_cv_loot } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cv_run value = 5 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c5_cash value = pe_cv_loot } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cv_run value = 6 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c6_cash value = pe_cv_loot } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cv_run value = 7 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c7_cash value = pe_cv_loot } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cv_run value = 8 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c8_cash value = pe_cv_loot } }
			}
			if = { limit = { is_ai = no } country_event = { id = pe.288 } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = pe_convoy_q value > 0 } }
			exists = event_target:pe_hold
			NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_convoy_cd }
			any_system_within_border = { pe_pirate_system = yes }
		}
		set_variable = { which = pe_cv_k value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_cv_best value = 599 }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c1_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c1_cash value > pe_cv_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_cv_k value = 1 } set_variable = { which = pe_cv_best value = pe_c1_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c2_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c2_cash value > pe_cv_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_cv_k value = 2 } set_variable = { which = pe_cv_best value = pe_c2_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c3_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c3_cash value > pe_cv_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_cv_k value = 3 } set_variable = { which = pe_cv_best value = pe_c3_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c4_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c4_cash value > pe_cv_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_cv_k value = 4 } set_variable = { which = pe_cv_best value = pe_c4_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c5_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c5_cash value > pe_cv_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_cv_k value = 5 } set_variable = { which = pe_cv_best value = pe_c5_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c6_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c6_cash value > pe_cv_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_cv_k value = 6 } set_variable = { which = pe_cv_best value = pe_c6_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c7_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c7_cash value > pe_cv_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_cv_k value = 7 } set_variable = { which = pe_cv_best value = pe_c7_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c8_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c8_cash value > pe_cv_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_cv_k value = 8 } set_variable = { which = pe_cv_best value = pe_c8_cash } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cv_k value > 0 } }
			random_list = {
				35 = {
					set_timed_country_flag = { flag = pe_convoy_cd days = 1080 }
					random_system_within_border = {
						limit = { pe_pirate_system = yes }
						save_event_target_as = pe_cv_sys
						save_event_target_as = pe_cv_loc
					}
					set_variable = { which = pe_cv_power value = 0 }
					event_target:pe_cv_sys = {
						every_fleet_in_system = {
							limit = { owner = { OR = { is_country_type = pirate is_country_type = marauder_raiders } } }
							export_trigger_value_to_variable = { trigger = fleet_power variable = pe_tmp }
							root = { change_variable = { which = pe_cv_power value = prev.pe_tmp } }
						}
					}
					set_variable = { which = pe_cv_need value = pe_cv_power }
					multiply_variable = { which = pe_cv_need value = 1.5 }
					set_variable = { which = pe_cv_budget value = pe_cv_best }
					multiply_variable = { which = pe_cv_budget value = 0.8 }
					if = {
						limit = { is_ai = yes }
						random = { chance = 80 pe_corp_convoy_start = yes }
					}
					else = { country_event = { id = pe.287 } }
				}
				65 = { modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { which = pe_fl_force value = 1 } } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# Country scope: start the convoy protection.
pe_corp_convoy_start = {
	set_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 1 }
	if = { limit = { has_technology = tech_destroyers } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 2 } }
	if = { limit = { has_technology = tech_cruisers } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 3 } }
	if = { limit = { has_technology = tech_battleships } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 4 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_ccost value = 150 } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_cprice value = 40 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_ccost value = 300 } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_cprice value = 80 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_ccost value = 600 } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_cprice value = 160 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 4 } } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_ccost value = 1200 } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_cprice value = 320 } }
	set_variable = { which = pe_cv_spent value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = pe_cv_ships value = 0 }
	create_fleet = {
		name = pe_corp_fleet_name
		settings = { uses_naval_capacity = no }
		effect = {
			set_owner = root
			set_variable = { which = pe_fp value = 0 }
			while = {
				count = 12
				limit = {
					check_variable = { which = pe_fp value < root.pe_cv_need }
					root = { check_variable = { which = pe_cv_spent value < pe_cv_budget } }
				}
				if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 1 } } } create_ship = { name = random random_existing_design = corvette } }
				else_if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 2 } } } create_ship = { name = random random_existing_design = destroyer } }
				else_if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 3 } } } create_ship = { name = random random_existing_design = cruiser } }
				else_if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 4 } } } create_ship = { name = random random_existing_design = battleship } }
				root = {
					change_variable = { which = pe_cv_spent value = pe_fl_ccost }
					change_variable = { which = pe_cv_ships value = 1 }
				}
				export_trigger_value_to_variable = { trigger = fleet_power variable = pe_fp }
			}
			set_location = event_target:pe_cv_loc
			set_fleet_flag = pe_leased
			set_fleet_flag = pe_lease_convoy
		}
	}
	# The leader is the holdings' own; assigned while they own and control the fleet, then leased.
	event_target:pe_hold = {
		create_leader = { class = commander species = root name = random skill = 2 }
		last_created_leader = { save_event_target_as = pe_fl_leader }
	}
	last_created_fleet = {
		set_owner = event_target:pe_hold
		assign_leader = event_target:pe_fl_leader
		set_controller = root
		add_modifier = { modifier = pe_leased_fleet days = -1 }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { exists = leader } } log = "private_economy: corporate fleet without a leader (assign_leader failed)" }
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cv_k value = 1 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c1_cash value = pe_cv_spent } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cv_k value = 2 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c2_cash value = pe_cv_spent } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cv_k value = 3 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c3_cash value = pe_cv_spent } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cv_k value = 4 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c4_cash value = pe_cv_spent } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cv_k value = 5 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c5_cash value = pe_cv_spent } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cv_k value = 6 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c6_cash value = pe_cv_spent } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cv_k value = 7 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c7_cash value = pe_cv_spent } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_cv_k value = 8 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c8_cash value = pe_cv_spent } }
	set_variable = { which = pe_convoy_q value = 12 }
	set_variable = { which = pe_cv_run value = pe_cv_k }
	event_target:pe_cv_sys = { set_star_flag = pe_convoy@root }
}

# --- Salvage (pe.289) -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Wrecks from battles (debris projects): a corporation with 300 cash offers to salvage them all.
# We get 80 alloys and 40 engineering research per field at once, the corporation 200 cash per
# field; the projects go to the holdings. 30% a quarter, then two years' pause.
pe_corp_salvage = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:pe_hold
			NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_salvage_cd }
			any_available_debris = { NOT = { must_research = yes } }
		}
		set_variable = { which = pe_sv_k value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_sv_best value = 299 }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c1_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c1_cash value > pe_sv_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_sv_k value = 1 } set_variable = { which = pe_sv_best value = pe_c1_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c2_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c2_cash value > pe_sv_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_sv_k value = 2 } set_variable = { which = pe_sv_best value = pe_c2_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c3_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c3_cash value > pe_sv_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_sv_k value = 3 } set_variable = { which = pe_sv_best value = pe_c3_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c4_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c4_cash value > pe_sv_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_sv_k value = 4 } set_variable = { which = pe_sv_best value = pe_c4_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c5_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c5_cash value > pe_sv_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_sv_k value = 5 } set_variable = { which = pe_sv_best value = pe_c5_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c6_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c6_cash value > pe_sv_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_sv_k value = 6 } set_variable = { which = pe_sv_best value = pe_c6_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c7_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c7_cash value > pe_sv_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_sv_k value = 7 } set_variable = { which = pe_sv_best value = pe_c7_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c8_on value = 1 }  check_variable = { which = pe_c8_cash value > pe_sv_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_sv_k value = 8 } set_variable = { which = pe_sv_best value = pe_c8_cash } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sv_k value > 0 } }
			random_list = {
				30 = {
					set_timed_country_flag = { flag = pe_salvage_cd days = 720 }
					set_variable = { which = pe_db_n value = 0 }
					every_available_debris = { limit = { NOT = { must_research = yes } } root = { change_variable = { which = pe_db_n value = 1 } } }
					set_variable = { which = pe_db_alloys value = pe_db_n }
					multiply_variable = { which = pe_db_alloys value = 80 }
					set_variable = { which = pe_db_res value = pe_db_n }
					multiply_variable = { which = pe_db_res value = 40 }
					set_variable = { which = pe_db_cash value = pe_db_n }
					multiply_variable = { which = pe_db_cash value = 200 }
					if = {
						limit = { is_ai = yes }
						random = { chance = 60 pe_corp_salvage_exec = yes }
					}
					else = { country_event = { id = pe.289 } }
				}
				70 = { modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { which = pe_fl_force value = 1 } } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# Country scope: the salvage deal.
pe_corp_salvage_exec = {
	every_available_debris = {
		limit = { NOT = { must_research = yes } }
		pass_debris_ownership = { owner = event_target:pe_hold }
	}
	add_resource = { alloys = 1 mult = pe_db_alloys }
	add_resource = { engineering_research = 1 mult = pe_db_res }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sv_k value = 1 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c1_cash value = pe_db_cash } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sv_k value = 2 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c2_cash value = pe_db_cash } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sv_k value = 3 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c3_cash value = pe_db_cash } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sv_k value = 4 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c4_cash value = pe_db_cash } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sv_k value = 5 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c5_cash value = pe_db_cash } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sv_k value = 6 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c6_cash value = pe_db_cash } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sv_k value = 7 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c7_cash value = pe_db_cash } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sv_k value = 8 } } change_variable = { which = pe_c8_cash value = pe_db_cash } }
}

# --- Dyson swarm (pe.292) -----------------------------------------------------------------------
# With the Dyson swarm technology, an energy or raw materials corporation with 3000 cash builds
# a swarm of its own (stage I) at a single star within our borders: under Laissez-faire without
# asking, under Free Market and Mixed by offer. It belongs to the holdings; its effect on the
# star's station is ours, and the corporation counts it as a station worth 20 a month
# (pe_c<slot>_sw), so it produces 40 more in its own product. One per corporation; 20% a
# quarter, then four years' pause.
pe_corp_swarm = {
	# Corporations with a swarm: a swarm that is gone (dismantled, destroyed, outside our
	# borders) ends it; a station at its star (older saves) is dismantled, like vanilla does;
	# the swarm is upgraded when the corporation can pay (stage II 4000, stage III 8000 with
	# the Dyson sphere technology; vanilla build time). Never a Dyson sphere.
	# The upkeep (vanilla alloys 5 / 10 / 20 a month by stage) is paid by the corporation from the
	# swarm's output: the holdings get the alloys for the next three months, their value (4 each,
	# pe_c<slot>_swu per month) is taken off what it delivers us (pe_corp_station_output).
	if = {
		limit = { exists = event_target:pe_hold NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_fl_debug } }
		set_variable = { which = pe_c1_sws value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_c1_swu value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c1_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c1_sw value = 1 } }
			set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 0 }
			random_planet_within_border = {
				limit = { has_planet_flag = pe_swc_1 }
				if = { limit = { has_modifier = dyson_swarm_1_mod } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 15 } set_variable = { which = pe_c1_sws value = 1 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { has_modifier = dyson_swarm_2_mod } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 30 } set_variable = { which = pe_c1_sws value = 2 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { has_modifier = dyson_swarm_3_mod } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 60 } set_variable = { which = pe_c1_sws value = 3 } } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value > 0 } }
				event_target:pe_hold = { add_resource = { alloys = 1 mult = root.pe_sw_upk } }
				set_variable = { which = pe_tmp value = pe_sw_upk }
				multiply_variable = { which = pe_tmp value = 4 }
				divide_variable = { which = pe_tmp value = 3 }
				set_variable = { which = pe_c1_swu value = pe_tmp }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { which = pe_c2_sws value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_c2_swu value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c2_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c2_sw value = 1 } }
			set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 0 }
			random_planet_within_border = {
				limit = { has_planet_flag = pe_swc_2 }
				if = { limit = { has_modifier = dyson_swarm_1_mod } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 15 } set_variable = { which = pe_c2_sws value = 1 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { has_modifier = dyson_swarm_2_mod } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 30 } set_variable = { which = pe_c2_sws value = 2 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { has_modifier = dyson_swarm_3_mod } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 60 } set_variable = { which = pe_c2_sws value = 3 } } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value > 0 } }
				event_target:pe_hold = { add_resource = { alloys = 1 mult = root.pe_sw_upk } }
				set_variable = { which = pe_tmp value = pe_sw_upk }
				multiply_variable = { which = pe_tmp value = 4 }
				divide_variable = { which = pe_tmp value = 3 }
				set_variable = { which = pe_c2_swu value = pe_tmp }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { which = pe_c3_sws value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_c3_swu value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c3_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c3_sw value = 1 } }
			set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 0 }
			random_planet_within_border = {
				limit = { has_planet_flag = pe_swc_3 }
				if = { limit = { has_modifier = dyson_swarm_1_mod } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 15 } set_variable = { which = pe_c3_sws value = 1 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { has_modifier = dyson_swarm_2_mod } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 30 } set_variable = { which = pe_c3_sws value = 2 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { has_modifier = dyson_swarm_3_mod } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 60 } set_variable = { which = pe_c3_sws value = 3 } } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value > 0 } }
				event_target:pe_hold = { add_resource = { alloys = 1 mult = root.pe_sw_upk } }
				set_variable = { which = pe_tmp value = pe_sw_upk }
				multiply_variable = { which = pe_tmp value = 4 }
				divide_variable = { which = pe_tmp value = 3 }
				set_variable = { which = pe_c3_swu value = pe_tmp }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { which = pe_c4_sws value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_c4_swu value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c4_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c4_sw value = 1 } }
			set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 0 }
			random_planet_within_border = {
				limit = { has_planet_flag = pe_swc_4 }
				if = { limit = { has_modifier = dyson_swarm_1_mod } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 15 } set_variable = { which = pe_c4_sws value = 1 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { has_modifier = dyson_swarm_2_mod } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 30 } set_variable = { which = pe_c4_sws value = 2 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { has_modifier = dyson_swarm_3_mod } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 60 } set_variable = { which = pe_c4_sws value = 3 } } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value > 0 } }
				event_target:pe_hold = { add_resource = { alloys = 1 mult = root.pe_sw_upk } }
				set_variable = { which = pe_tmp value = pe_sw_upk }
				multiply_variable = { which = pe_tmp value = 4 }
				divide_variable = { which = pe_tmp value = 3 }
				set_variable = { which = pe_c4_swu value = pe_tmp }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { which = pe_c5_sws value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_c5_swu value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c5_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c5_sw value = 1 } }
			set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 0 }
			random_planet_within_border = {
				limit = { has_planet_flag = pe_swc_5 }
				if = { limit = { has_modifier = dyson_swarm_1_mod } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 15 } set_variable = { which = pe_c5_sws value = 1 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { has_modifier = dyson_swarm_2_mod } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 30 } set_variable = { which = pe_c5_sws value = 2 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { has_modifier = dyson_swarm_3_mod } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 60 } set_variable = { which = pe_c5_sws value = 3 } } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value > 0 } }
				event_target:pe_hold = { add_resource = { alloys = 1 mult = root.pe_sw_upk } }
				set_variable = { which = pe_tmp value = pe_sw_upk }
				multiply_variable = { which = pe_tmp value = 4 }
				divide_variable = { which = pe_tmp value = 3 }
				set_variable = { which = pe_c5_swu value = pe_tmp }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { which = pe_c6_sws value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_c6_swu value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c6_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c6_sw value = 1 } }
			set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 0 }
			random_planet_within_border = {
				limit = { has_planet_flag = pe_swc_6 }
				if = { limit = { has_modifier = dyson_swarm_1_mod } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 15 } set_variable = { which = pe_c6_sws value = 1 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { has_modifier = dyson_swarm_2_mod } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 30 } set_variable = { which = pe_c6_sws value = 2 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { has_modifier = dyson_swarm_3_mod } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 60 } set_variable = { which = pe_c6_sws value = 3 } } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value > 0 } }
				event_target:pe_hold = { add_resource = { alloys = 1 mult = root.pe_sw_upk } }
				set_variable = { which = pe_tmp value = pe_sw_upk }
				multiply_variable = { which = pe_tmp value = 4 }
				divide_variable = { which = pe_tmp value = 3 }
				set_variable = { which = pe_c6_swu value = pe_tmp }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { which = pe_c7_sws value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_c7_swu value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c7_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c7_sw value = 1 } }
			set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 0 }
			random_planet_within_border = {
				limit = { has_planet_flag = pe_swc_7 }
				if = { limit = { has_modifier = dyson_swarm_1_mod } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 15 } set_variable = { which = pe_c7_sws value = 1 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { has_modifier = dyson_swarm_2_mod } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 30 } set_variable = { which = pe_c7_sws value = 2 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { has_modifier = dyson_swarm_3_mod } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 60 } set_variable = { which = pe_c7_sws value = 3 } } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value > 0 } }
				event_target:pe_hold = { add_resource = { alloys = 1 mult = root.pe_sw_upk } }
				set_variable = { which = pe_tmp value = pe_sw_upk }
				multiply_variable = { which = pe_tmp value = 4 }
				divide_variable = { which = pe_tmp value = 3 }
				set_variable = { which = pe_c7_swu value = pe_tmp }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { which = pe_c8_sws value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_c8_swu value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c8_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c8_sw value = 1 } }
			set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 0 }
			random_planet_within_border = {
				limit = { has_planet_flag = pe_swc_8 }
				if = { limit = { has_modifier = dyson_swarm_1_mod } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 15 } set_variable = { which = pe_c8_sws value = 1 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { has_modifier = dyson_swarm_2_mod } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 30 } set_variable = { which = pe_c8_sws value = 2 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { has_modifier = dyson_swarm_3_mod } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value = 60 } set_variable = { which = pe_c8_sws value = 3 } } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sw_upk value > 0 } }
				event_target:pe_hold = { add_resource = { alloys = 1 mult = root.pe_sw_upk } }
				set_variable = { which = pe_tmp value = pe_sw_upk }
				multiply_variable = { which = pe_tmp value = 4 }
				divide_variable = { which = pe_tmp value = 3 }
				set_variable = { which = pe_c8_swu value = pe_tmp }
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c1_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c1_sw value = 1 } exists = event_target:pe_hold }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { any_planet_within_border = { has_planet_flag = pe_swc_1 OR = { has_planet_flag = pe_swb has_modifier = dyson_swarm_1_mod has_modifier = dyson_swarm_2_mod has_modifier = dyson_swarm_3_mod } } } }
			set_variable = { which = pe_c1_sw value = 0 }
		}
		random_planet_within_border = {
			limit = { has_planet_flag = pe_swc_1 has_orbital_station = yes }
			orbital_station = { dismantle = yes }
		}
		set_variable = { which = pe_sw_up value = 0 }
		event_target:pe_hold = {

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c3_on value = 1 } OR = { check_variable = { which = pe_c3_prod value = 9 } check_variable = { which = pe_c3_grp value = 4 } } NOT = { check_variable = { which = pe_c3_sw value = 1 } } check_variable = { which = pe_c3_cash value > pe_sw_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_sw_k value = 3 } set_variable = { which = pe_sw_best value = pe_c3_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c4_on value = 1 } OR = { check_variable = { which = pe_c4_prod value = 9 } check_variable = { which = pe_c4_grp value = 4 } } NOT = { check_variable = { which = pe_c4_sw value = 1 } } check_variable = { which = pe_c4_cash value > pe_sw_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_sw_k value = 4 } set_variable = { which = pe_sw_best value = pe_c4_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c5_on value = 1 } OR = { check_variable = { which = pe_c5_prod value = 9 } check_variable = { which = pe_c5_grp value = 4 } } NOT = { check_variable = { which = pe_c5_sw value = 1 } } check_variable = { which = pe_c5_cash value > pe_sw_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_sw_k value = 5 } set_variable = { which = pe_sw_best value = pe_c5_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c6_on value = 1 } OR = { check_variable = { which = pe_c6_prod value = 9 } check_variable = { which = pe_c6_grp value = 4 } } NOT = { check_variable = { which = pe_c6_sw value = 1 } } check_variable = { which = pe_c6_cash value > pe_sw_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_sw_k value = 6 } set_variable = { which = pe_sw_best value = pe_c6_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c7_on value = 1 } OR = { check_variable = { which = pe_c7_prod value = 9 } check_variable = { which = pe_c7_grp value = 4 } } NOT = { check_variable = { which = pe_c7_sw value = 1 } } check_variable = { which = pe_c7_cash value > pe_sw_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_sw_k value = 7 } set_variable = { which = pe_sw_best value = pe_c7_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c8_on value = 1 } OR = { check_variable = { which = pe_c8_prod value = 9 } check_variable = { which = pe_c8_grp value = 4 } } NOT = { check_variable = { which = pe_c8_sw value = 1 } } check_variable = { which = pe_c8_cash value > pe_sw_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_sw_k value = 8 } set_variable = { which = pe_sw_best value = pe_c8_cash } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sw_k value > 0 } }
			random_list = {
				20 = {
					set_timed_country_flag = { flag = pe_swarm_cd days = 1440 }
					# The star: of those whose deposit pays the upkeep at the last stage (the
					# corporation earns half the value a month: value x 31 / 2 >= 80 with the Dyson
					# sphere technology, else value x 16 / 2 >= 40), a middling one (pe_select_mid).
					set_variable = { which = pe_sw_thr value = 5 }
					if = { limit = { has_technology = tech_dyson_sphere } set_variable = { which = pe_sw_thr value = 5.17 } }
					if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_force value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_sw_thr value = 0.1 } }
					set_variable = { which = pe_sel_n value = 0 }
					set_variable = { which = pe_sel_sum value = 0 }
					set_variable = { which = pe_sel_min value = 1000000 }
					set_variable = { which = pe_sel_max value = -1 }
					every_planet_within_border = {
						limit = { pe_swarm_star = yes solar_system = { pe_swarm_system = yes } }
						pe_orbital_value = yes
						set_variable = { which = pe_sel_v value = pe_ov }
						set_variable = { which = pe_sel_c value = 0 }
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sel_v value >= root.pe_sw_thr } }
							set_variable = { which = pe_sel_c value = 1 }
							root = {
								change_variable = { which = pe_sel_n value = 1 }
								change_variable = { which = pe_sel_sum value = prev.pe_sel_v }
								if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sel_min value > prev.pe_sel_v } } set_variable = { which = pe_sel_min value = prev.pe_sel_v } }
								if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sel_max value < prev.pe_sel_v } } set_variable = { which = pe_sel_max value = prev.pe_sel_v } }
							}
						}
					}
					set_variable = { which = pe_sel_found value = 0 }
					if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sel_n value > 0 } } pe_select_mid = yes }
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sel_found value = 1 } }
						event_target:pe_sel_p = {
							save_event_target_as = pe_sw_star
							solar_system = { save_event_target_as = pe_sw_sys }
						}
						if = {
							limit = { OR = { has_policy_flag = pe_order_laissez_faire is_ai = yes } }
							pe_corp_swarm_exec = yes
						}
						else = { country_event = { id = pe.292 } }
					}
				}
				80 = { modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { which = pe_fl_force value = 1 } } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# Country scope: the corporation starts its swarm at event_target:pe_sw_star.
pe_corp_swarm_exec = {
	# Like vanilla, the swarm replaces the station at the star: a station of ours is dismantled
	# and paid for (Laissez-faire the building cost, otherwise twice that).
	set_variable = { which = pe_sw_pay value = 0 }
	event_target:pe_sw_star = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_orbital_station = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { pe_state_station = yes }
				root = {
					set_variable = { which = pe_sw_pay value = 100 }
					if = { limit = { NOT = { has_policy_flag = pe_order_laissez_faire } } set_variable = { which = pe_sw_pay value = 200 } }
				}
			}
			orbital_station = { dismantle = yes }
		}
	}

# Country scope, quarterly: corporate arc furnaces (Laissez-faire, Free Market, Mixed; with the
# vanilla technology). First the corporations' furnaces: a furnace that is gone takes its
# deposits and the holdings' stations with it; otherwise the corporation sets its smelting
# process (industry alloys, raw materials minerals; from stage III), pays the upkeep and
# upgrades when it can pay. Then, every four years at most, a new furnace: a corporation of
# industry or raw materials with 2500 cash builds one (cost 2000) over a molten planet. The
# holdings get a station on every body of the system that had no deposit before; bodies with
# a deposit stay ours (and get the furnace deposit and bonus too). The site: one whose bodies
# for the holdings pay the upkeep at stage IV (the corporation earns half the value a month:
# alloys 12 per body against 125 energy, so at least 11 bodies; minerals 6 per body against
# 75, at least 13), and of those a middling one (pe_select_mid).
pe_corp_furnace = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c1_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c1_af value = 1 } exists = event_target:pe_hold }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_upg value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 1 }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c1_grp value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 3 } }
		event_target:pe_hold = {
			every_owned_megastructure = {
				limit = { planet = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 1 } } }
				if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_1 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 1 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_2 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 2 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_3 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 3 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_4 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 4 } } }
				if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = pe_corp_furnace_site } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = -1 } } }
				if = { limit = { is_upgrading = yes } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_upg value = 1 } } }
				if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_minerals has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_minerals } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_balanced has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_balanced } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 2 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_alloys has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_alloys } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_obsessional_directive has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_obsessional_directive } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 4 } } }
				# The corporation's smelting process: industry alloys, raw materials minerals.
				if = {
					limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value >= 3 } NOT = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = pe_af_want } } } }
					if = {
						limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_3 }
						if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 3 } } } set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_alloys }
						else = { set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_minerals }
					}
					else = {
						if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 3 } } } set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_alloys }
						else = { set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_minerals }
					}
					root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = pe_af_want } }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { which = pe_c1_afs value = pe_af_st }
		set_variable = { which = pe_c1_afu value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 0 } }
			# The furnace is gone: its deposits, bonuses and the holdings' stations go too.
			set_variable = { which = pe_c1_af value = 0 }
			every_planet_within_border = {
				limit = { is_variable_set = pe_af_corp check_variable = { which = pe_af_corp value = 1 } }
				dismantle_arc_furnace_effect = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 1 } pe_holdings_station = yes }
					orbital_station = { dismantle = yes }
				}
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_corp value = 0 }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 0 }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = 0 }
			}
			every_planet_within_border = { limit = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 0 } }
		}
		# Still being built (pe_af_st -1): nothing yet.
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value > 0 } }
			# Its deposit per body for the holdings' stations (station bonus of the stage included):
			# I 1.25 minerals, II 3; III 7 minerals or 3.5 alloys; IV 12 minerals or 6 alloys.
			set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 0 }
			set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 1.25 } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 3 } }
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 3 } }
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 7 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 3.5 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 1.75 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 3.5 } }
			}
			else = {
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 12 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 4 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 4 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 6 } }
			}
			every_planet_within_border = {
				limit = { is_variable_set = pe_af_corp check_variable = { which = pe_af_corp value = 1 } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_1 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_1 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_2 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_2 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_3 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_3 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_4 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_4 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				# Older saves (0.26.0): the furnace deposit came twice, and the bodies without a
				# deposit before (only the furnace deposit now) got no station of the holdings.
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 0 } num_deposits = 1 has_orbital_station = no NOT = { has_planet_flag = has_arc_furnace } }
					set_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 1 }
					create_mining_station = { owner = event_target:pe_hold }
					if = { limit = { has_orbital_station = yes } orbital_station = { set_fleet_flag = pe_stc_1 } }
				}
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_min value = root.pe_af_vm }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_al value = root.pe_af_va }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = pe_af_al }
				multiply_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = 4 }
				change_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = pe_af_min }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_fl_debug } }
				# The upkeep for the next three months (vanilla energy 20 / 40 / 80 / 100 a month;
				# from stage III minerals -25%, alloys +25%), paid by the corporation from the output:
				# taken off what it delivers us (pe_c<slot>_afu per month, pe_corp_station_output).
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 60 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 120 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 240 } }
				else = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 300 } }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value >= 3 } }
					if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } multiply_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 0.75 } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } multiply_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 1.25 } }
				}
				event_target:pe_hold = { add_resource = { energy = 1 mult = root.pe_af_upk } }
				set_variable = { which = pe_c1_afu value = pe_af_upk }
				divide_variable = { which = pe_c1_afu value = 3 }
				# The next stage when the corporation can pay it (II 4000, III 7000, IV 12000).
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 0 }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_upg value = 0 } }
					event_target:pe_hold = {
						every_owned_megastructure = {
							limit = { is_upgrading = no planet = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 1 } } }
							if = {
								limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_1 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_c1_cash value >= 4000 } } }
								upgrade_megastructure_to = orbital_arc_furnace_2
								root = { change_variable = { which = pe_c1_cash value = -4000 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 2 } }
							}
