							else_if = {
								limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_2 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_c1_cash value >= 7000 } } }
								upgrade_megastructure_to = orbital_arc_furnace_3
								root = { change_variable = { which = pe_c1_cash value = -7000 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 3 } }
							}
							else_if = {
								limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_3 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_c1_cash value >= 12000 } } }
								upgrade_megastructure_to = orbital_arc_furnace_4
								root = { change_variable = { which = pe_c1_cash value = -12000 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 4 } }
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_up value > 0 } is_ai = no }
					set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c1_name }
					random_planet_within_border = { limit = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 1 } } save_event_target_as = pe_msg_planet }
					pe_corp_msg = { KEY = pe_msg_corp_furnace_up }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c2_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c2_af value = 1 } exists = event_target:pe_hold }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_upg value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 1 }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c2_grp value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 3 } }
		event_target:pe_hold = {
			every_owned_megastructure = {
				limit = { planet = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 2 } } }
				if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_1 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 1 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_2 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 2 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_3 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 3 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_4 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 4 } } }
				if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = pe_corp_furnace_site } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = -1 } } }
				if = { limit = { is_upgrading = yes } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_upg value = 1 } } }
				if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_minerals has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_minerals } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_balanced has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_balanced } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 2 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_alloys has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_alloys } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_obsessional_directive has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_obsessional_directive } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 4 } } }
				# The corporation's smelting process: industry alloys, raw materials minerals.
				if = {
					limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value >= 3 } NOT = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = pe_af_want } } } }
					if = {
						limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_3 }
						if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 3 } } } set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_alloys }
						else = { set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_minerals }
					}
					else = {
						if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 3 } } } set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_alloys }
						else = { set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_minerals }
					}
					root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = pe_af_want } }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { which = pe_c2_afs value = pe_af_st }
		set_variable = { which = pe_c2_afu value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 0 } }
			# The furnace is gone: its deposits, bonuses and the holdings' stations go too.
			set_variable = { which = pe_c2_af value = 0 }
			every_planet_within_border = {
				limit = { is_variable_set = pe_af_corp check_variable = { which = pe_af_corp value = 2 } }
				dismantle_arc_furnace_effect = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 1 } pe_holdings_station = yes }
					orbital_station = { dismantle = yes }
				}
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_corp value = 0 }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 0 }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = 0 }
			}
			every_planet_within_border = { limit = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 0 } }
		}
		# Still being built (pe_af_st -1): nothing yet.
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value > 0 } }
			# Its deposit per body for the holdings' stations (station bonus of the stage included):
			# I 1.25 minerals, II 3; III 7 minerals or 3.5 alloys; IV 12 minerals or 6 alloys.
			set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 0 }
			set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 1.25 } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 3 } }
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 3 } }
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 7 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 3.5 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 1.75 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 3.5 } }
			}
			else = {
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 12 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 4 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 4 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 6 } }
			}
			every_planet_within_border = {
				limit = { is_variable_set = pe_af_corp check_variable = { which = pe_af_corp value = 2 } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_1 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_1 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_2 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_2 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_3 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_3 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_4 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_4 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				# Older saves (0.26.0): the furnace deposit came twice, and the bodies without a
				# deposit before (only the furnace deposit now) got no station of the holdings.
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 0 } num_deposits = 1 has_orbital_station = no NOT = { has_planet_flag = has_arc_furnace } }
					set_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 1 }
					create_mining_station = { owner = event_target:pe_hold }
					if = { limit = { has_orbital_station = yes } orbital_station = { set_fleet_flag = pe_stc_2 } }
				}
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_min value = root.pe_af_vm }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_al value = root.pe_af_va }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = pe_af_al }
				multiply_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = 4 }
				change_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = pe_af_min }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_fl_debug } }
				# The upkeep for the next three months (vanilla energy 20 / 40 / 80 / 100 a month;
				# from stage III minerals -25%, alloys +25%), paid by the corporation from the output:
				# taken off what it delivers us (pe_c<slot>_afu per month, pe_corp_station_output).
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 60 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 120 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 240 } }
				else = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 300 } }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value >= 3 } }
					if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } multiply_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 0.75 } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } multiply_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 1.25 } }
				}
				event_target:pe_hold = { add_resource = { energy = 1 mult = root.pe_af_upk } }
				set_variable = { which = pe_c2_afu value = pe_af_upk }
				divide_variable = { which = pe_c2_afu value = 3 }
				# The next stage when the corporation can pay it (II 4000, III 7000, IV 12000).
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 0 }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_upg value = 0 } }
					event_target:pe_hold = {
						every_owned_megastructure = {
							limit = { is_upgrading = no planet = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 2 } } }
							if = {
								limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_1 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_c2_cash value >= 4000 } } }
								upgrade_megastructure_to = orbital_arc_furnace_2
								root = { change_variable = { which = pe_c2_cash value = -4000 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 2 } }
							}
							else_if = {
								limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_2 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_c2_cash value >= 7000 } } }
								upgrade_megastructure_to = orbital_arc_furnace_3
								root = { change_variable = { which = pe_c2_cash value = -7000 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 3 } }
							}
							else_if = {
								limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_3 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_c2_cash value >= 12000 } } }
								upgrade_megastructure_to = orbital_arc_furnace_4
								root = { change_variable = { which = pe_c2_cash value = -12000 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 4 } }
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_up value > 0 } is_ai = no }
					set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c2_name }
					random_planet_within_border = { limit = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 2 } } save_event_target_as = pe_msg_planet }
					pe_corp_msg = { KEY = pe_msg_corp_furnace_up }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c3_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c3_af value = 1 } exists = event_target:pe_hold }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_upg value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 1 }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c3_grp value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 3 } }
		event_target:pe_hold = {
			every_owned_megastructure = {
				limit = { planet = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 3 } } }
				if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_1 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 1 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_2 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 2 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_3 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 3 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_4 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 4 } } }
				if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = pe_corp_furnace_site } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = -1 } } }
				if = { limit = { is_upgrading = yes } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_upg value = 1 } } }
				if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_minerals has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_minerals } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_balanced has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_balanced } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 2 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_alloys has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_alloys } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_obsessional_directive has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_obsessional_directive } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 4 } } }
				# The corporation's smelting process: industry alloys, raw materials minerals.
				if = {
					limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value >= 3 } NOT = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = pe_af_want } } } }
					if = {
						limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_3 }
						if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 3 } } } set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_alloys }
						else = { set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_minerals }
					}
					else = {
						if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 3 } } } set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_alloys }
						else = { set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_minerals }
					}
					root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = pe_af_want } }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { which = pe_c3_afs value = pe_af_st }
		set_variable = { which = pe_c3_afu value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 0 } }
			# The furnace is gone: its deposits, bonuses and the holdings' stations go too.
			set_variable = { which = pe_c3_af value = 0 }
			every_planet_within_border = {
				limit = { is_variable_set = pe_af_corp check_variable = { which = pe_af_corp value = 3 } }
				dismantle_arc_furnace_effect = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 1 } pe_holdings_station = yes }
					orbital_station = { dismantle = yes }
				}
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_corp value = 0 }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 0 }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = 0 }
			}
			every_planet_within_border = { limit = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 0 } }
		}
		# Still being built (pe_af_st -1): nothing yet.
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value > 0 } }
			# Its deposit per body for the holdings' stations (station bonus of the stage included):
			# I 1.25 minerals, II 3; III 7 minerals or 3.5 alloys; IV 12 minerals or 6 alloys.
			set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 0 }
			set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 1.25 } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 3 } }
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 3 } }
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 7 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 3.5 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 1.75 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 3.5 } }
			}
			else = {
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 12 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 4 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 4 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 6 } }
			}
			every_planet_within_border = {
				limit = { is_variable_set = pe_af_corp check_variable = { which = pe_af_corp value = 3 } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_1 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_1 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_2 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_2 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_3 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_3 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_4 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_4 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				# Older saves (0.26.0): the furnace deposit came twice, and the bodies without a
				# deposit before (only the furnace deposit now) got no station of the holdings.
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 0 } num_deposits = 1 has_orbital_station = no NOT = { has_planet_flag = has_arc_furnace } }
					set_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 1 }
					create_mining_station = { owner = event_target:pe_hold }
					if = { limit = { has_orbital_station = yes } orbital_station = { set_fleet_flag = pe_stc_3 } }
				}
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_min value = root.pe_af_vm }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_al value = root.pe_af_va }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = pe_af_al }
				multiply_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = 4 }
				change_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = pe_af_min }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_fl_debug } }
				# The upkeep for the next three months (vanilla energy 20 / 40 / 80 / 100 a month;
				# from stage III minerals -25%, alloys +25%), paid by the corporation from the output:
				# taken off what it delivers us (pe_c<slot>_afu per month, pe_corp_station_output).
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 60 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 120 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 240 } }
				else = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 300 } }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value >= 3 } }
					if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } multiply_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 0.75 } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } multiply_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 1.25 } }
				}
				event_target:pe_hold = { add_resource = { energy = 1 mult = root.pe_af_upk } }
				set_variable = { which = pe_c3_afu value = pe_af_upk }
				divide_variable = { which = pe_c3_afu value = 3 }
				# The next stage when the corporation can pay it (II 4000, III 7000, IV 12000).
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 0 }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_upg value = 0 } }
					event_target:pe_hold = {
						every_owned_megastructure = {
							limit = { is_upgrading = no planet = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 3 } } }
							if = {
								limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_1 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_c3_cash value >= 4000 } } }
								upgrade_megastructure_to = orbital_arc_furnace_2
								root = { change_variable = { which = pe_c3_cash value = -4000 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 2 } }
							}
							else_if = {
								limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_2 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_c3_cash value >= 7000 } } }
								upgrade_megastructure_to = orbital_arc_furnace_3
								root = { change_variable = { which = pe_c3_cash value = -7000 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 3 } }
							}
							else_if = {
								limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_3 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_c3_cash value >= 12000 } } }
								upgrade_megastructure_to = orbital_arc_furnace_4
								root = { change_variable = { which = pe_c3_cash value = -12000 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 4 } }
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_up value > 0 } is_ai = no }
					set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c3_name }
					random_planet_within_border = { limit = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 3 } } save_event_target_as = pe_msg_planet }
					pe_corp_msg = { KEY = pe_msg_corp_furnace_up }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c4_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c4_af value = 1 } exists = event_target:pe_hold }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_upg value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 1 }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c4_grp value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 3 } }
		event_target:pe_hold = {
			every_owned_megastructure = {
				limit = { planet = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 4 } } }
				if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_1 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 1 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_2 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 2 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_3 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 3 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_4 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 4 } } }
				if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = pe_corp_furnace_site } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = -1 } } }
				if = { limit = { is_upgrading = yes } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_upg value = 1 } } }
				if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_minerals has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_minerals } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_balanced has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_balanced } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 2 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_alloys has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_alloys } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_obsessional_directive has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_obsessional_directive } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 4 } } }
				# The corporation's smelting process: industry alloys, raw materials minerals.
				if = {
					limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value >= 3 } NOT = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = pe_af_want } } } }
					if = {
						limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_3 }
						if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 3 } } } set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_alloys }
						else = { set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_minerals }
					}
					else = {
						if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 3 } } } set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_alloys }
						else = { set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_minerals }
					}
					root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = pe_af_want } }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { which = pe_c4_afs value = pe_af_st }
		set_variable = { which = pe_c4_afu value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 0 } }
			# The furnace is gone: its deposits, bonuses and the holdings' stations go too.
			set_variable = { which = pe_c4_af value = 0 }
			every_planet_within_border = {
				limit = { is_variable_set = pe_af_corp check_variable = { which = pe_af_corp value = 4 } }
				dismantle_arc_furnace_effect = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 1 } pe_holdings_station = yes }
					orbital_station = { dismantle = yes }
				}
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_corp value = 0 }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 0 }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = 0 }
			}
			every_planet_within_border = { limit = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 4 } } set_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 0 } }
		}
		# Still being built (pe_af_st -1): nothing yet.
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value > 0 } }
			# Its deposit per body for the holdings' stations (station bonus of the stage included):
			# I 1.25 minerals, II 3; III 7 minerals or 3.5 alloys; IV 12 minerals or 6 alloys.
			set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 0 }
			set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 1.25 } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 3 } }
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 3 } }
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 7 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 3.5 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 1.75 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 3.5 } }
			}
			else = {
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 12 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 4 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 4 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 6 } }
			}
			every_planet_within_border = {
				limit = { is_variable_set = pe_af_corp check_variable = { which = pe_af_corp value = 4 } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_1 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_1 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_2 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_2 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_3 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_3 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_4 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_4 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				# Older saves (0.26.0): the furnace deposit came twice, and the bodies without a
				# deposit before (only the furnace deposit now) got no station of the holdings.
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 0 } num_deposits = 1 has_orbital_station = no NOT = { has_planet_flag = has_arc_furnace } }
					set_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 1 }
					create_mining_station = { owner = event_target:pe_hold }
					if = { limit = { has_orbital_station = yes } orbital_station = { set_fleet_flag = pe_stc_4 } }
				}
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_min value = root.pe_af_vm }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_al value = root.pe_af_va }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = pe_af_al }
				multiply_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = 4 }
				change_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = pe_af_min }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_fl_debug } }
				# The upkeep for the next three months (vanilla energy 20 / 40 / 80 / 100 a month;
				# from stage III minerals -25%, alloys +25%), paid by the corporation from the output:
				# taken off what it delivers us (pe_c<slot>_afu per month, pe_corp_station_output).
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 60 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 120 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 240 } }
				else = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 300 } }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value >= 3 } }
					if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } multiply_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 0.75 } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } multiply_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 1.25 } }
				}
				event_target:pe_hold = { add_resource = { energy = 1 mult = root.pe_af_upk } }
				set_variable = { which = pe_c4_afu value = pe_af_upk }
				divide_variable = { which = pe_c4_afu value = 3 }
				# The next stage when the corporation can pay it (II 4000, III 7000, IV 12000).
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 0 }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_upg value = 0 } }
					event_target:pe_hold = {
						every_owned_megastructure = {
							limit = { is_upgrading = no planet = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 4 } } }
							if = {
								limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_1 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_c4_cash value >= 4000 } } }
								upgrade_megastructure_to = orbital_arc_furnace_2
								root = { change_variable = { which = pe_c4_cash value = -4000 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 2 } }
							}
							else_if = {
								limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_2 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_c4_cash value >= 7000 } } }
								upgrade_megastructure_to = orbital_arc_furnace_3
								root = { change_variable = { which = pe_c4_cash value = -7000 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 3 } }
							}
							else_if = {
								limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_3 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_c4_cash value >= 12000 } } }
								upgrade_megastructure_to = orbital_arc_furnace_4
								root = { change_variable = { which = pe_c4_cash value = -12000 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 4 } }
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_up value > 0 } is_ai = no }
					set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c4_name }
					random_planet_within_border = { limit = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 4 } } save_event_target_as = pe_msg_planet }
					pe_corp_msg = { KEY = pe_msg_corp_furnace_up }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c5_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c5_af value = 1 } exists = event_target:pe_hold }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_upg value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 1 }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c5_grp value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 3 } }
		event_target:pe_hold = {
			every_owned_megastructure = {
				limit = { planet = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 5 } } }
				if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_1 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 1 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_2 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 2 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_3 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 3 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_4 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 4 } } }
				if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = pe_corp_furnace_site } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = -1 } } }
				if = { limit = { is_upgrading = yes } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_upg value = 1 } } }
				if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_minerals has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_minerals } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_balanced has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_balanced } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 2 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_alloys has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_alloys } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_obsessional_directive has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_obsessional_directive } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 4 } } }
				# The corporation's smelting process: industry alloys, raw materials minerals.
				if = {
					limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value >= 3 } NOT = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = pe_af_want } } } }
					if = {
						limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_3 }
						if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 3 } } } set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_alloys }
						else = { set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_minerals }
					}
					else = {
						if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 3 } } } set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_alloys }
						else = { set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_minerals }
					}
					root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = pe_af_want } }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { which = pe_c5_afs value = pe_af_st }
		set_variable = { which = pe_c5_afu value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 0 } }
			# The furnace is gone: its deposits, bonuses and the holdings' stations go too.
			set_variable = { which = pe_c5_af value = 0 }
			every_planet_within_border = {
				limit = { is_variable_set = pe_af_corp check_variable = { which = pe_af_corp value = 5 } }
				dismantle_arc_furnace_effect = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 1 } pe_holdings_station = yes }
					orbital_station = { dismantle = yes }
				}
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_corp value = 0 }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 0 }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = 0 }
			}
			every_planet_within_border = { limit = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 5 } } set_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 0 } }
		}
		# Still being built (pe_af_st -1): nothing yet.
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value > 0 } }
			# Its deposit per body for the holdings' stations (station bonus of the stage included):
			# I 1.25 minerals, II 3; III 7 minerals or 3.5 alloys; IV 12 minerals or 6 alloys.
			set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 0 }
			set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 1.25 } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 3 } }
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 3 } }
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 7 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 3.5 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 1.75 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 3.5 } }
			}
			else = {
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 12 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 4 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 4 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 6 } }
			}
			every_planet_within_border = {
				limit = { is_variable_set = pe_af_corp check_variable = { which = pe_af_corp value = 5 } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_1 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_1 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_2 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_2 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_3 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_3 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_4 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_4 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				# Older saves (0.26.0): the furnace deposit came twice, and the bodies without a
				# deposit before (only the furnace deposit now) got no station of the holdings.
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 0 } num_deposits = 1 has_orbital_station = no NOT = { has_planet_flag = has_arc_furnace } }
					set_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 1 }
					create_mining_station = { owner = event_target:pe_hold }
					if = { limit = { has_orbital_station = yes } orbital_station = { set_fleet_flag = pe_stc_5 } }
				}
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_min value = root.pe_af_vm }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_al value = root.pe_af_va }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = pe_af_al }
				multiply_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = 4 }
				change_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = pe_af_min }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_fl_debug } }
				# The upkeep for the next three months (vanilla energy 20 / 40 / 80 / 100 a month;
				# from stage III minerals -25%, alloys +25%), paid by the corporation from the output:
				# taken off what it delivers us (pe_c<slot>_afu per month, pe_corp_station_output).
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 60 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 120 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 240 } }
				else = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 300 } }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value >= 3 } }
					if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } multiply_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 0.75 } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } multiply_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 1.25 } }
				}
				event_target:pe_hold = { add_resource = { energy = 1 mult = root.pe_af_upk } }
				set_variable = { which = pe_c5_afu value = pe_af_upk }
				divide_variable = { which = pe_c5_afu value = 3 }
				# The next stage when the corporation can pay it (II 4000, III 7000, IV 12000).
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 0 }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_upg value = 0 } }
					event_target:pe_hold = {
						every_owned_megastructure = {
							limit = { is_upgrading = no planet = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 5 } } }
							if = {
								limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_1 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_c5_cash value >= 4000 } } }
								upgrade_megastructure_to = orbital_arc_furnace_2
								root = { change_variable = { which = pe_c5_cash value = -4000 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 2 } }
							}
							else_if = {
								limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_2 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_c5_cash value >= 7000 } } }
								upgrade_megastructure_to = orbital_arc_furnace_3
								root = { change_variable = { which = pe_c5_cash value = -7000 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 3 } }
							}
							else_if = {
								limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_3 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_c5_cash value >= 12000 } } }
								upgrade_megastructure_to = orbital_arc_furnace_4
								root = { change_variable = { which = pe_c5_cash value = -12000 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 4 } }
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_up value > 0 } is_ai = no }
					set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c5_name }
					random_planet_within_border = { limit = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 5 } } save_event_target_as = pe_msg_planet }
					pe_corp_msg = { KEY = pe_msg_corp_furnace_up }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c6_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c6_af value = 1 } exists = event_target:pe_hold }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_upg value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 1 }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c6_grp value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 3 } }
		event_target:pe_hold = {
			every_owned_megastructure = {
				limit = { planet = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 6 } } }
				if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_1 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 1 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_2 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 2 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_3 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 3 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_4 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 4 } } }
				if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = pe_corp_furnace_site } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = -1 } } }
				if = { limit = { is_upgrading = yes } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_upg value = 1 } } }
				if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_minerals has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_minerals } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_balanced has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_balanced } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 2 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_alloys has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_alloys } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_obsessional_directive has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_obsessional_directive } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 4 } } }
				# The corporation's smelting process: industry alloys, raw materials minerals.
				if = {
					limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value >= 3 } NOT = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = pe_af_want } } } }
					if = {
						limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_3 }
						if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 3 } } } set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_alloys }
						else = { set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_minerals }
					}
					else = {
						if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 3 } } } set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_alloys }
						else = { set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_minerals }
					}
					root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = pe_af_want } }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { which = pe_c6_afs value = pe_af_st }
		set_variable = { which = pe_c6_afu value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 0 } }
			# The furnace is gone: its deposits, bonuses and the holdings' stations go too.
			set_variable = { which = pe_c6_af value = 0 }
			every_planet_within_border = {
				limit = { is_variable_set = pe_af_corp check_variable = { which = pe_af_corp value = 6 } }
				dismantle_arc_furnace_effect = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 1 } pe_holdings_station = yes }
					orbital_station = { dismantle = yes }
				}
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_corp value = 0 }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 0 }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = 0 }
			}
			every_planet_within_border = { limit = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 6 } } set_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 0 } }
		}
		# Still being built (pe_af_st -1): nothing yet.
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value > 0 } }
			# Its deposit per body for the holdings' stations (station bonus of the stage included):
			# I 1.25 minerals, II 3; III 7 minerals or 3.5 alloys; IV 12 minerals or 6 alloys.
			set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 0 }
			set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 1.25 } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 3 } }
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 3 } }
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 7 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 3.5 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 1.75 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 3.5 } }
			}
			else = {
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 12 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 4 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 4 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 6 } }
			}
			every_planet_within_border = {
				limit = { is_variable_set = pe_af_corp check_variable = { which = pe_af_corp value = 6 } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_1 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_1 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_2 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_2 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_3 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_3 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_4 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_4 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				# Older saves (0.26.0): the furnace deposit came twice, and the bodies without a
				# deposit before (only the furnace deposit now) got no station of the holdings.
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 0 } num_deposits = 1 has_orbital_station = no NOT = { has_planet_flag = has_arc_furnace } }
					set_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 1 }
					create_mining_station = { owner = event_target:pe_hold }
					if = { limit = { has_orbital_station = yes } orbital_station = { set_fleet_flag = pe_stc_6 } }
				}
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_min value = root.pe_af_vm }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_al value = root.pe_af_va }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = pe_af_al }
				multiply_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = 4 }
				change_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = pe_af_min }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_fl_debug } }
				# The upkeep for the next three months (vanilla energy 20 / 40 / 80 / 100 a month;
				# from stage III minerals -25%, alloys +25%), paid by the corporation from the output:
				# taken off what it delivers us (pe_c<slot>_afu per month, pe_corp_station_output).
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 60 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 120 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 240 } }
				else = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 300 } }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value >= 3 } }
					if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } multiply_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 0.75 } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } multiply_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 1.25 } }
				}
				event_target:pe_hold = { add_resource = { energy = 1 mult = root.pe_af_upk } }
				set_variable = { which = pe_c6_afu value = pe_af_upk }
				divide_variable = { which = pe_c6_afu value = 3 }
				# The next stage when the corporation can pay it (II 4000, III 7000, IV 12000).
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 0 }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_upg value = 0 } }
					event_target:pe_hold = {
						every_owned_megastructure = {
							limit = { is_upgrading = no planet = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 6 } } }
							if = {
								limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_1 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_c6_cash value >= 4000 } } }
								upgrade_megastructure_to = orbital_arc_furnace_2
								root = { change_variable = { which = pe_c6_cash value = -4000 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 2 } }
							}
							else_if = {
								limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_2 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_c6_cash value >= 7000 } } }
								upgrade_megastructure_to = orbital_arc_furnace_3
								root = { change_variable = { which = pe_c6_cash value = -7000 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 3 } }
							}
							else_if = {
								limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_3 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_c6_cash value >= 12000 } } }
								upgrade_megastructure_to = orbital_arc_furnace_4
								root = { change_variable = { which = pe_c6_cash value = -12000 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 4 } }
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_up value > 0 } is_ai = no }
					set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c6_name }
					random_planet_within_border = { limit = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 6 } } save_event_target_as = pe_msg_planet }
					pe_corp_msg = { KEY = pe_msg_corp_furnace_up }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c7_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c7_af value = 1 } exists = event_target:pe_hold }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_upg value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 1 }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c7_grp value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 3 } }
		event_target:pe_hold = {
			every_owned_megastructure = {
				limit = { planet = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 7 } } }
				if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_1 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 1 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_2 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 2 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_3 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 3 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_4 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 4 } } }
				if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = pe_corp_furnace_site } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = -1 } } }
				if = { limit = { is_upgrading = yes } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_upg value = 1 } } }
				if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_minerals has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_minerals } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_balanced has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_balanced } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 2 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_alloys has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_alloys } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_obsessional_directive has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_obsessional_directive } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 4 } } }
				# The corporation's smelting process: industry alloys, raw materials minerals.
				if = {
					limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value >= 3 } NOT = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = pe_af_want } } } }
					if = {
						limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_3 }
						if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 3 } } } set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_alloys }
						else = { set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_minerals }
					}
					else = {
						if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 3 } } } set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_alloys }
						else = { set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_minerals }
					}
					root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = pe_af_want } }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { which = pe_c7_afs value = pe_af_st }
		set_variable = { which = pe_c7_afu value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 0 } }
			# The furnace is gone: its deposits, bonuses and the holdings' stations go too.
			set_variable = { which = pe_c7_af value = 0 }
			every_planet_within_border = {
				limit = { is_variable_set = pe_af_corp check_variable = { which = pe_af_corp value = 7 } }
				dismantle_arc_furnace_effect = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 1 } pe_holdings_station = yes }
					orbital_station = { dismantle = yes }
				}
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_corp value = 0 }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 0 }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = 0 }
			}
			every_planet_within_border = { limit = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 7 } } set_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 0 } }
		}
		# Still being built (pe_af_st -1): nothing yet.
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value > 0 } }
			# Its deposit per body for the holdings' stations (station bonus of the stage included):
			# I 1.25 minerals, II 3; III 7 minerals or 3.5 alloys; IV 12 minerals or 6 alloys.
			set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 0 }
			set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 1.25 } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 3 } }
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 3 } }
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 7 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 3.5 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 1.75 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 3.5 } }
			}
			else = {
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 12 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 4 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 4 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 6 } }
			}
			every_planet_within_border = {
				limit = { is_variable_set = pe_af_corp check_variable = { which = pe_af_corp value = 7 } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_1 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_1 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_2 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_2 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_3 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_3 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_4 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_4 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				# Older saves (0.26.0): the furnace deposit came twice, and the bodies without a
				# deposit before (only the furnace deposit now) got no station of the holdings.
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 0 } num_deposits = 1 has_orbital_station = no NOT = { has_planet_flag = has_arc_furnace } }
					set_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 1 }
					create_mining_station = { owner = event_target:pe_hold }
					if = { limit = { has_orbital_station = yes } orbital_station = { set_fleet_flag = pe_stc_7 } }
				}
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_min value = root.pe_af_vm }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_al value = root.pe_af_va }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = pe_af_al }
				multiply_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = 4 }
				change_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = pe_af_min }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_fl_debug } }
				# The upkeep for the next three months (vanilla energy 20 / 40 / 80 / 100 a month;
				# from stage III minerals -25%, alloys +25%), paid by the corporation from the output:
				# taken off what it delivers us (pe_c<slot>_afu per month, pe_corp_station_output).
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 60 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 120 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 240 } }
				else = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 300 } }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value >= 3 } }
					if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } multiply_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 0.75 } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } multiply_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 1.25 } }
				}
				event_target:pe_hold = { add_resource = { energy = 1 mult = root.pe_af_upk } }
				set_variable = { which = pe_c7_afu value = pe_af_upk }
				divide_variable = { which = pe_c7_afu value = 3 }
				# The next stage when the corporation can pay it (II 4000, III 7000, IV 12000).
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 0 }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_upg value = 0 } }
					event_target:pe_hold = {
						every_owned_megastructure = {
							limit = { is_upgrading = no planet = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 7 } } }
							if = {
								limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_1 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_c7_cash value >= 4000 } } }
								upgrade_megastructure_to = orbital_arc_furnace_2
								root = { change_variable = { which = pe_c7_cash value = -4000 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 2 } }
							}
							else_if = {
								limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_2 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_c7_cash value >= 7000 } } }
								upgrade_megastructure_to = orbital_arc_furnace_3
								root = { change_variable = { which = pe_c7_cash value = -7000 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 3 } }
							}
							else_if = {
								limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_3 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_c7_cash value >= 12000 } } }
								upgrade_megastructure_to = orbital_arc_furnace_4
								root = { change_variable = { which = pe_c7_cash value = -12000 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 4 } }
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_up value > 0 } is_ai = no }
					set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c7_name }
					random_planet_within_border = { limit = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 7 } } save_event_target_as = pe_msg_planet }
					pe_corp_msg = { KEY = pe_msg_corp_furnace_up }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c8_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c8_af value = 1 } exists = event_target:pe_hold }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_upg value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 1 }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c8_grp value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 3 } }
		event_target:pe_hold = {
			every_owned_megastructure = {
				limit = { planet = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 8 } } }
				if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_1 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 1 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_2 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 2 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_3 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 3 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_4 } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 4 } } }
				if = { limit = { is_megastructure_type = pe_corp_furnace_site } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = -1 } } }
				if = { limit = { is_upgrading = yes } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_upg value = 1 } } }
				if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_minerals has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_minerals } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_balanced has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_balanced } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 2 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_alloys has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_alloys } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } }
				else_if = { limit = { OR = { has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_obsessional_directive has_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_obsessional_directive } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 4 } } }
				# The corporation's smelting process: industry alloys, raw materials minerals.
				if = {
					limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value >= 3 } NOT = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = pe_af_want } } } }
					if = {
						limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_3 }
						if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 3 } } } set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_alloys }
						else = { set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_3_minerals }
					}
					else = {
						if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_want value = 3 } } } set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_alloys }
						else = { set_overclock = overclock_arc_furnace_4_minerals }
					}
					root = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = pe_af_want } }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { which = pe_c8_afs value = pe_af_st }
		set_variable = { which = pe_c8_afu value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 0 } }
			# The furnace is gone: its deposits, bonuses and the holdings' stations go too.
			set_variable = { which = pe_c8_af value = 0 }
			every_planet_within_border = {
				limit = { is_variable_set = pe_af_corp check_variable = { which = pe_af_corp value = 8 } }
				dismantle_arc_furnace_effect = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 1 } pe_holdings_station = yes }
					orbital_station = { dismantle = yes }
				}
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_corp value = 0 }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 0 }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = 0 }
			}
			every_planet_within_border = { limit = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 8 } } set_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 0 } }
		}
		# Still being built (pe_af_st -1): nothing yet.
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value > 0 } }
			# Its deposit per body for the holdings' stations (station bonus of the stage included):
			# I 1.25 minerals, II 3; III 7 minerals or 3.5 alloys; IV 12 minerals or 6 alloys.
			set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 0 }
			set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 1.25 } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 3 } }
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 3 } }
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 7 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 3.5 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 1.75 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 3.5 } }
			}
			else = {
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 12 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_vm value = 4 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 4 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_va value = 6 } }
			}
			every_planet_within_border = {
				limit = { is_variable_set = pe_af_corp check_variable = { which = pe_af_corp value = 8 } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_1 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_1 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_2 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_2 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_3 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_3 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				if = { limit = { count_deposits = { type = d_arc_furnace_4 count > 1 } } random_deposit = { limit = { is_deposit_type = d_arc_furnace_4 } remove_deposit = yes } }
				# Older saves (0.26.0): the furnace deposit came twice, and the bodies without a
				# deposit before (only the furnace deposit now) got no station of the holdings.
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 0 } num_deposits = 1 has_orbital_station = no NOT = { has_planet_flag = has_arc_furnace } }
					set_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 1 }
					create_mining_station = { owner = event_target:pe_hold }
					if = { limit = { has_orbital_station = yes } orbital_station = { set_fleet_flag = pe_stc_8 } }
				}
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_min value = root.pe_af_vm }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_al value = root.pe_af_va }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = pe_af_al }
				multiply_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = 4 }
				change_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = pe_af_min }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_fl_debug } }
				# The upkeep for the next three months (vanilla energy 20 / 40 / 80 / 100 a month;
				# from stage III minerals -25%, alloys +25%), paid by the corporation from the output:
				# taken off what it delivers us (pe_c<slot>_afu per month, pe_corp_station_output).
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 60 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 120 } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 240 } }
				else = { set_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 300 } }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_st value >= 3 } }
					if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 1 } } multiply_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 0.75 } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_oc value = 3 } } multiply_variable = { which = pe_af_upk value = 1.25 } }
				}
				event_target:pe_hold = { add_resource = { energy = 1 mult = root.pe_af_upk } }
				set_variable = { which = pe_c8_afu value = pe_af_upk }
				divide_variable = { which = pe_c8_afu value = 3 }
				# The next stage when the corporation can pay it (II 4000, III 7000, IV 12000).
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 0 }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_upg value = 0 } }
					event_target:pe_hold = {
						every_owned_megastructure = {
							limit = { is_upgrading = no planet = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 8 } } }
							if = {
								limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_1 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_c8_cash value >= 4000 } } }
								upgrade_megastructure_to = orbital_arc_furnace_2
								root = { change_variable = { which = pe_c8_cash value = -4000 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 2 } }
							}
							else_if = {
								limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_2 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_c8_cash value >= 7000 } } }
								upgrade_megastructure_to = orbital_arc_furnace_3
								root = { change_variable = { which = pe_c8_cash value = -7000 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 3 } }
							}
							else_if = {
								limit = { is_megastructure_type = orbital_arc_furnace_3 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_c8_cash value >= 12000 } } }
								upgrade_megastructure_to = orbital_arc_furnace_4
								root = { change_variable = { which = pe_c8_cash value = -12000 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_up value = 4 } }
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_up value > 0 } is_ai = no }
					set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c8_name }
					random_planet_within_border = { limit = { is_variable_set = pe_afc check_variable = { which = pe_afc value = 8 } } save_event_target_as = pe_msg_planet }
					pe_corp_msg = { KEY = pe_msg_corp_furnace_up }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:pe_hold
			has_technology = tech_orbital_arc_furnace
			OR = { has_policy_flag = pe_order_laissez_faire has_policy_flag = pe_order_free_market has_policy_flag = pe_order_mixed }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_furnace_cd }
			any_planet_within_border = { pe_furnace_site = yes }
		}
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_af_best value = 2499 }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c1_on value = 1 } OR = { check_variable = { which = pe_c1_grp value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c1_grp value = 4 } } NOT = { check_variable = { which = pe_c1_af value = 1 } } check_variable = { which = pe_c1_cash value > pe_af_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 1 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_best value = pe_c1_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c2_on value = 1 } OR = { check_variable = { which = pe_c2_grp value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c2_grp value = 4 } } NOT = { check_variable = { which = pe_c2_af value = 1 } } check_variable = { which = pe_c2_cash value > pe_af_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 2 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_best value = pe_c2_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c3_on value = 1 } OR = { check_variable = { which = pe_c3_grp value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c3_grp value = 4 } } NOT = { check_variable = { which = pe_c3_af value = 1 } } check_variable = { which = pe_c3_cash value > pe_af_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 3 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_best value = pe_c3_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c4_on value = 1 } OR = { check_variable = { which = pe_c4_grp value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c4_grp value = 4 } } NOT = { check_variable = { which = pe_c4_af value = 1 } } check_variable = { which = pe_c4_cash value > pe_af_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 4 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_best value = pe_c4_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c5_on value = 1 } OR = { check_variable = { which = pe_c5_grp value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c5_grp value = 4 } } NOT = { check_variable = { which = pe_c5_af value = 1 } } check_variable = { which = pe_c5_cash value > pe_af_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 5 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_best value = pe_c5_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c6_on value = 1 } OR = { check_variable = { which = pe_c6_grp value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c6_grp value = 4 } } NOT = { check_variable = { which = pe_c6_af value = 1 } } check_variable = { which = pe_c6_cash value > pe_af_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 6 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_best value = pe_c6_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c7_on value = 1 } OR = { check_variable = { which = pe_c7_grp value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c7_grp value = 4 } } NOT = { check_variable = { which = pe_c7_af value = 1 } } check_variable = { which = pe_c7_cash value > pe_af_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 7 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_best value = pe_c7_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c8_on value = 1 } OR = { check_variable = { which = pe_c8_grp value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c8_grp value = 4 } } NOT = { check_variable = { which = pe_c8_af value = 1 } } check_variable = { which = pe_c8_cash value > pe_af_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 8 } set_variable = { which = pe_af_best value = pe_c8_cash } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value > 0 } }
			random_list = {
				20 = {
					set_timed_country_flag = { flag = pe_furnace_cd days = 1440 }
					set_variable = { which = pe_af_thr value = 13 }
					if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c1_grp value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_thr value = 11 } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 2 } check_variable = { which = pe_c2_grp value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_thr value = 11 } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 3 } check_variable = { which = pe_c3_grp value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_thr value = 11 } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 4 } check_variable = { which = pe_c4_grp value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_thr value = 11 } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 5 } check_variable = { which = pe_c5_grp value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_thr value = 11 } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 6 } check_variable = { which = pe_c6_grp value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_thr value = 11 } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 7 } check_variable = { which = pe_c7_grp value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_thr value = 11 } }
					else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 8 } check_variable = { which = pe_c8_grp value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_thr value = 11 } }
					if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_force value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_thr value = 1 } }
					set_variable = { which = pe_sel_n value = 0 }
					set_variable = { which = pe_sel_sum value = 0 }
					set_variable = { which = pe_sel_min value = 1000000 }
					set_variable = { which = pe_sel_max value = -1 }
					every_planet_within_border = {
						limit = { pe_furnace_site = yes }
						# Bodies for the holdings: they get the furnace deposit and had none before.
						set_variable = { which = pe_sel_v value = 0 }
						set_variable = { which = pe_sel_c value = 0 }
						solar_system = {
							every_system_planet = {
								limit = { NOT = { has_deposit_for = shipclass_research_station } is_star = no is_astral_scar = no colonizable_planet = no NOT = { has_deposit_for = shipclass_mining_station } NOT = { is_same_value = prevprev } }
								prevprev = { change_variable = { which = pe_sel_v value = 1 } }
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sel_v value >= root.pe_af_thr } }
							set_variable = { which = pe_sel_c value = 1 }
							root = {
								change_variable = { which = pe_sel_n value = 1 }
								change_variable = { which = pe_sel_sum value = prev.pe_sel_v }
								if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sel_min value > prev.pe_sel_v } } set_variable = { which = pe_sel_min value = prev.pe_sel_v } }
								if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sel_max value < prev.pe_sel_v } } set_variable = { which = pe_sel_max value = prev.pe_sel_v } }
							}
						}
