					}
					set_variable = { which = pe_sel_found value = 0 }
					if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sel_n value > 0 } } pe_select_mid = yes }
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sel_found value = 1 } }
						event_target:pe_sel_p = { save_event_target_as = pe_af_p }
						if = {
							limit = { OR = { has_policy_flag = pe_order_laissez_faire is_ai = yes } }
							pe_corp_furnace_exec = yes
						}
						else = { country_event = { id = pe.293 } }
					}
				}
				80 = { modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { which = pe_fl_force value = 1 } } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# Country scope: the corporation pe_af_k starts its furnace over event_target:pe_af_p. Built like
# vanilla: a site of the holdings, built up with the vanilla build time of a furnace; when it is
# done, pe.305 finishes it (pe_corp_furnace_finish). pe_afb meanwhile.
pe_corp_furnace_exec = {
	# A station of ours there is dismantled and paid for (Laissez-faire the building cost,
	# otherwise twice that).
	set_variable = { which = pe_af_pay value = 0 }
	event_target:pe_af_p = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_orbital_station = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { pe_state_station = yes }
				root = {
					set_variable = { which = pe_af_pay value = 100 }
					if = { limit = { NOT = { has_policy_flag = pe_order_laissez_faire } } set_variable = { which = pe_af_pay value = 200 } }
				}
			}
			orbital_station = { dismantle = yes }
		}
	}
	add_resource = { minerals = 1 mult = pe_af_pay }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 1 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c1_cash value = pe_af_pay } change_variable = { which = pe_c1_cash value = -2000 } set_variable = { which = pe_c1_af value = 1 } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c1_name } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 2 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c2_cash value = pe_af_pay } change_variable = { which = pe_c2_cash value = -2000 } set_variable = { which = pe_c2_af value = 1 } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c2_name } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 3 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c3_cash value = pe_af_pay } change_variable = { which = pe_c3_cash value = -2000 } set_variable = { which = pe_c3_af value = 1 } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c3_name } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 4 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c4_cash value = pe_af_pay } change_variable = { which = pe_c4_cash value = -2000 } set_variable = { which = pe_c4_af value = 1 } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c4_name } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 5 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c5_cash value = pe_af_pay } change_variable = { which = pe_c5_cash value = -2000 } set_variable = { which = pe_c5_af value = 1 } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c5_name } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 6 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c6_cash value = pe_af_pay } change_variable = { which = pe_c6_cash value = -2000 } set_variable = { which = pe_c6_af value = 1 } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c6_name } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 7 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c7_cash value = pe_af_pay } change_variable = { which = pe_c7_cash value = -2000 } set_variable = { which = pe_c7_af value = 1 } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c7_name } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 8 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c8_cash value = pe_af_pay } change_variable = { which = pe_c8_cash value = -2000 } set_variable = { which = pe_c8_af value = 1 } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c8_name } }
	event_target:pe_af_p = {
		set_planet_flag = has_megastructure
		set_planet_flag = pe_afb
		set_variable = { which = pe_afc value = root.pe_af_k }
		save_event_target_as = pe_msg_planet
		solar_system = {
			spawn_megastructure = {
				type = pe_corp_furnace_site
				owner = event_target:pe_hold
				planet = event_target:pe_af_p
			}
		}
	}
	event_target:pe_hold = {
		every_owned_megastructure = {
			limit = { is_megastructure_type = pe_corp_furnace_site is_upgrading = no }
			upgrade_megastructure_to = pe_corp_furnace_build
		}
	}
	# The message inline (call depth).
	if = {
		limit = { is_ai = no }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = 1 } } create_message = { type = pe_private_economy localization = pe_msg_corp_furnace_start days = 30 target = event_target:pe_msg_planet variable = { type = name localization = PLANET scope = event_target:pe_msg_planet } variable = { type = key value = pe_cname_1 localization = CORP } } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = 2 } } create_message = { type = pe_private_economy localization = pe_msg_corp_furnace_start days = 30 target = event_target:pe_msg_planet variable = { type = name localization = PLANET scope = event_target:pe_msg_planet } variable = { type = key value = pe_cname_2 localization = CORP } } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = 3 } } create_message = { type = pe_private_economy localization = pe_msg_corp_furnace_start days = 30 target = event_target:pe_msg_planet variable = { type = name localization = PLANET scope = event_target:pe_msg_planet } variable = { type = key value = pe_cname_3 localization = CORP } } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = 4 } } create_message = { type = pe_private_economy localization = pe_msg_corp_furnace_start days = 30 target = event_target:pe_msg_planet variable = { type = name localization = PLANET scope = event_target:pe_msg_planet } variable = { type = key value = pe_cname_4 localization = CORP } } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = 5 } } create_message = { type = pe_private_economy localization = pe_msg_corp_furnace_start days = 30 target = event_target:pe_msg_planet variable = { type = name localization = PLANET scope = event_target:pe_msg_planet } variable = { type = key value = pe_cname_5 localization = CORP } } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = 6 } } create_message = { type = pe_private_economy localization = pe_msg_corp_furnace_start days = 30 target = event_target:pe_msg_planet variable = { type = name localization = PLANET scope = event_target:pe_msg_planet } variable = { type = key value = pe_cname_6 localization = CORP } } }

# Country scope (pe.305): the furnace site of corporation pe_af_k over event_target:pe_af_p is
# done: the furnace (stage I) for the holdings, as vanilla's on_build_complete and
# cybernetics.1000 would (the spawned furnace runs neither for us).
pe_corp_furnace_finish = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c1_name } } else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c2_name } } else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c3_name } } else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 4 } } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c4_name } } else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 5 } } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c5_name } } else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 6 } } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c6_name } } else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 7 } } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c7_name } } else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 8 } } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c8_name } }
	event_target:pe_af_p = {
		# The bodies without a deposit, before the furnace exists: the spawned furnace runs
		# vanilla's on_build_complete (cybernetics.1000) at once and gives them its deposit.
		solar_system = {
			every_system_planet = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_deposit_for = shipclass_research_station } is_star = no is_astral_scar = no colonizable_planet = no }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 0 }
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_deposit_for = shipclass_mining_station } NOT = { is_same_value = event_target:pe_af_p } }
					set_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 1 }
				}
			}
		}
		solar_system = {
			spawn_megastructure = {
				type = orbital_arc_furnace_1
				owner = event_target:pe_hold
				planet = event_target:pe_af_p
			}
		}
		set_planet_flag = has_megastructure
		set_planet_flag = has_arc_furnace
		set_planet_entity = { entity = invisible_turret_entity }
		set_variable = { which = pe_afc value = root.pe_af_k }
		solar_system = {
			every_system_planet = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_deposit_for = shipclass_research_station } is_star = no is_astral_scar = no colonizable_planet = no }
				# What vanilla's completion did, unless it did (remove_trade_deposits inline).
				remove_deposit = d_trade_value_1
				remove_deposit = d_trade_value_2
				remove_deposit = d_trade_value_3
				remove_deposit = d_trade_value_4
				remove_deposit = d_trade_value_5
				remove_deposit = d_trade_value_6
				remove_deposit = d_trade_value_7
				remove_deposit = d_trade_value_8
				remove_deposit = d_trade_value_9
				remove_deposit = d_trade_value_10
				if = { limit = { NOT = { has_deposit = d_arc_furnace_1 } } add_deposit = d_arc_furnace_1 }
				if = { limit = { NOT = { has_modifier = orbital_arc_furnace_1_mod } } add_modifier = { modifier = orbital_arc_furnace_1_mod days = -1 } }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_corp value = root.pe_af_k }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_min value = 1.25 }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_al value = 0 }
				set_variable = { which = pe_af_val value = 1.25 }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_h value = 1 } }
					if = { limit = { has_orbital_station = no } create_mining_station = { owner = event_target:pe_hold } }
					if = {
						limit = { has_orbital_station = yes }
						orbital_station = {
							if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 1 } } } set_fleet_flag = pe_stc_1 }
							else_if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 2 } } } set_fleet_flag = pe_stc_2 }
							else_if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 3 } } } set_fleet_flag = pe_stc_3 }
							else_if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 4 } } } set_fleet_flag = pe_stc_4 }
							else_if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 5 } } } set_fleet_flag = pe_stc_5 }
							else_if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 6 } } } set_fleet_flag = pe_stc_6 }
							else_if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 7 } } } set_fleet_flag = pe_stc_7 }
							else_if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_af_k value = 8 } } } set_fleet_flag = pe_stc_8 }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		save_event_target_as = pe_msg_planet
	}
	# The message inline (call depth).
	if = {
		limit = { is_ai = no }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = 1 } } create_message = { type = pe_private_economy localization = pe_msg_corp_furnace days = 30 target = event_target:pe_msg_planet variable = { type = name localization = PLANET scope = event_target:pe_msg_planet } variable = { type = key value = pe_cname_1 localization = CORP } } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = 2 } } create_message = { type = pe_private_economy localization = pe_msg_corp_furnace days = 30 target = event_target:pe_msg_planet variable = { type = name localization = PLANET scope = event_target:pe_msg_planet } variable = { type = key value = pe_cname_2 localization = CORP } } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = 3 } } create_message = { type = pe_private_economy localization = pe_msg_corp_furnace days = 30 target = event_target:pe_msg_planet variable = { type = name localization = PLANET scope = event_target:pe_msg_planet } variable = { type = key value = pe_cname_3 localization = CORP } } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = 4 } } create_message = { type = pe_private_economy localization = pe_msg_corp_furnace days = 30 target = event_target:pe_msg_planet variable = { type = name localization = PLANET scope = event_target:pe_msg_planet } variable = { type = key value = pe_cname_4 localization = CORP } } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = 5 } } create_message = { type = pe_private_economy localization = pe_msg_corp_furnace days = 30 target = event_target:pe_msg_planet variable = { type = name localization = PLANET scope = event_target:pe_msg_planet } variable = { type = key value = pe_cname_5 localization = CORP } } }

		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = 7 } } create_message = { type = pe_private_economy localization = pe_msg_corp_furnace days = 30 target = event_target:pe_msg_planet variable = { type = name localization = PLANET scope = event_target:pe_msg_planet } variable = { type = key value = pe_cname_7 localization = CORP } } }

		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = 111 } } create_message = { type = pe_private_economy localization = pe_msg_corp_furnace days = 30 target = event_target:pe_msg_planet variable = { type = name localization = PLANET scope = event_target:pe_msg_planet } variable = { type = key value = pe_cname_111 localization = CORP } } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = 112 } } create_message = { type = pe_private_economy localization = pe_msg_corp_furnace days = 30 target = event_target:pe_msg_planet variable = { type = name localization = PLANET scope = event_target:pe_msg_planet } variable = { type = key value = pe_cname_112 localization = CORP } } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = 113 } } create_message = { type = pe_private_economy localization = pe_msg_corp_furnace days = 30 target = event_target:pe_msg_planet variable = { type = name localization = PLANET scope = event_target:pe_msg_planet } variable = { type = key value = pe_cname_113 localization = CORP } } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = 114 } } create_message = { type = pe_private_economy localization = pe_msg_corp_furnace days = 30 target = event_target:pe_msg_planet variable = { type = name localization = PLANET scope = event_target:pe_msg_planet } variable = { type = key value = pe_cname_114 localization = CORP } } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = 115 } } create_message = { type = pe_private_economy localization = pe_msg_corp_furnace days = 30 target = event_target:pe_msg_planet variable = { type = name localization = PLANET scope = event_target:pe_msg_planet } variable = { type = key value = pe_cname_115 localization = CORP } } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = 116 } } create_message = { type = pe_private_economy localization = pe_msg_corp_furnace days = 30 target = event_target:pe_msg_planet variable = { type = name localization = PLANET scope = event_target:pe_msg_planet } variable = { type = key value = pe_cname_116 localization = CORP } } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = 117 } } create_message = { type = pe_private_economy localization = pe_msg_corp_furnace days = 30 target = event_target:pe_msg_planet variable = { type = name localization = PLANET scope = event_target:pe_msg_planet } variable = { type = key value = pe_cname_117 localization = CORP } } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = 118 } } create_message = { type = pe_private_economy localization = pe_msg_corp_furnace days = 30 target = event_target:pe_msg_planet variable = { type = name localization = PLANET scope = event_target:pe_msg_planet } variable = { type = key value = pe_cname_118 localization = CORP } } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = 119 } } create_message = { type = pe_private_economy localization = pe_msg_corp_furnace days = 30 target = event_target:pe_msg_planet variable = { type = name localization = PLANET scope = event_target:pe_msg_planet } variable = { type = key value = pe_cname_119 localization = CORP } } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = 120 } } create_message = { type = pe_private_economy localization = pe_msg_corp_furnace days = 30 target = event_target:pe_msg_planet variable = { type = name localization = PLANET scope = event_target:pe_msg_planet } variable = { type = key value = pe_cname_120 localization = CORP } } }
	}
}

# Country scope: of the candidate planets (pe_sel_c = 1, score pe_sel_v; pe_sel_n candidates,
# pe_sel_sum, pe_sel_min, pe_sel_max), the one closest to the average. With three or more
# candidates of different scores neither the best nor the worst. event_target:pe_sel_p,
# pe_sel_found = 1. Resets pe_sel_c.
pe_select_mid = {
	set_variable = { which = pe_sel_found value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = pe_sel_best value = 1000000 }
	set_variable = { which = pe_sel_mean value = pe_sel_sum }
	divide_variable = { which = pe_sel_mean value = pe_sel_n }
	set_variable = { which = pe_sel_ex value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sel_n value >= 3 } check_variable = { which = pe_sel_max value > pe_sel_min } }
		set_variable = { which = pe_sel_ex value = 1 }
	}
	every_planet_within_border = {
		limit = { is_variable_set = pe_sel_c check_variable = { which = pe_sel_c value = 1 } }
		set_variable = { which = pe_sel_d value = pe_sel_v }
		subtract_variable = { which = pe_sel_d value = root.pe_sel_mean }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sel_d value < 0 } } multiply_variable = { which = pe_sel_d value = -1 } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { which = pe_sel_d value < root.pe_sel_best }
				OR = {
					root = { check_variable = { which = pe_sel_ex value = 0 } }
					AND = {
						check_variable = { which = pe_sel_v value < root.pe_sel_max }
						check_variable = { which = pe_sel_v value > root.pe_sel_min }
					}
				}
			}
			root = { set_variable = { which = pe_sel_best value = prev.pe_sel_d } set_variable = { which = pe_sel_found value = 1 } }
			save_event_target_as = pe_sel_p
		}
	}
	# Nothing strictly between the best and the worst (e.g. 5, 5, 10): the closest of all.
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_sel_found value = 0 } }
		every_planet_within_border = {
			limit = { is_variable_set = pe_sel_c check_variable = { which = pe_sel_c value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_sel_d value < root.pe_sel_best } }
			root = { set_variable = { which = pe_sel_best value = prev.pe_sel_d } set_variable = { which = pe_sel_found value = 1 } }
			save_event_target_as = pe_sel_p
		}
	}
	every_planet_within_border = {
		limit = { is_variable_set = pe_sel_c check_variable = { which = pe_sel_c value = 1 } }
		set_variable = { which = pe_sel_c value = 0 }
	}
}

# --- Planetary defence (pe.294 / 295) --------------------------------------------------------
# Country scope, monthly (pe.100): one of our planets is bombarded (by monsters or an enemy). A
# strong corporation (at least 1500 cash, the one with the most) offers to send a fleet of its
# own at once, built from our designs, at its own cost (up to 80% of its cash, aiming at 1.5x
# the hostile fleet power in the system). It wants something big in return: the independent
# firms of the planet, alloys (an eighth of its budget) or concessions (150 influence); we may
# haggle (half the alloys, or it leaves). The fleet is held by our Private Holdings and under
# our command (so it fights in our wars), no lease, no fee; it withdraws when the system is
# clear and the bombardment over, after a year at the latest, or when it is destroyed. At most
# every two years. A bombarded planet with a corporation's headquarters: that corporation
# sends its fleet for free, down to its credit line (once a year). Variables pe_df_.
pe_corp_defence = {
	random_country = {
		limit = { is_country_type = pe_holdings has_relation_flag = { who = root flag = pe_holdings_of } }
		save_event_target_as = pe_hold
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_q value > 0 } NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_fl_debug } }
		change_variable = { which = pe_df_q value = -1 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_df_end value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { any_controlled_fleet = { has_fleet_flag = pe_lease_defence } } }
			set_variable = { which = pe_df_end value = 2 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_owned_planet = {
					has_planet_flag = pe_df_target
					has_orbital_bombardment = no
					NOT = { solar_system = { any_fleet_in_system = { owner = { OR = { is_hostile = root is_at_war_with = root } } fleet_power > 0 } } }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { which = pe_df_end value = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_q value <= 0 } }
			set_variable = { which = pe_df_end value = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_end value > 0 } }
			set_variable = { which = pe_df_q value = 0 }
			every_controlled_fleet = { limit = { has_fleet_flag = pe_lease_defence } if = { limit = { exists = leader } kill_leader = { show_notification = no fire = yes } } delete_fleet = this }
			every_owned_planet = { limit = { has_planet_flag = pe_df_target } save_event_target_as = pe_msg_planet remove_planet_flag = pe_df_target }
			if = {
				limit = { is_ai = no exists = event_target:pe_msg_planet }
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_run value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c1_name } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_run value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c2_name } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_run value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c3_name } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_run value = 4 } } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c4_name } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_run value = 5 } } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c5_name } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_run value = 6 } } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c6_name } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_run value = 7 } } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c7_name } }
				else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_run value = 8 } } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c8_name } }
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_end value = 1 } } pe_corp_msg = { KEY = pe_msg_defence_end } }
				else = { pe_corp_msg = { KEY = pe_msg_defence_lost } }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_q value > 0 } }
			exists = event_target:pe_hold
			any_owned_planet = { has_orbital_bombardment = yes }
		}
		set_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = pe_df_free value = 0 }
		# A bombarded headquarters: its corporation defends it for free, as long as it can pay,
		# down to its credit line (-200). At most once a year.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_defence_hq_cd } }
			random_owned_planet = {
				limit = { has_orbital_bombardment = yes OR = { has_planet_flag = pe_hq_1 has_planet_flag = pe_hq_2 has_planet_flag = pe_hq_3 has_planet_flag = pe_hq_4 has_planet_flag = pe_hq_5 has_planet_flag = pe_hq_6 has_planet_flag = pe_hq_7 has_planet_flag = pe_hq_8 } }
				save_event_target_as = pe_df_planet
				if = { limit = { has_planet_flag = pe_hq_1 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 0 } check_variable = { which = pe_c1_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c1_cash value >= -50 } } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 1 } } }
				if = { limit = { has_planet_flag = pe_hq_2 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 0 } check_variable = { which = pe_c2_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c2_cash value >= -50 } } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 2 } } }
				if = { limit = { has_planet_flag = pe_hq_3 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 0 } check_variable = { which = pe_c3_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c3_cash value >= -50 } } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 3 } } }
				if = { limit = { has_planet_flag = pe_hq_4 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 0 } check_variable = { which = pe_c4_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c4_cash value >= -50 } } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 4 } } }
				if = { limit = { has_planet_flag = pe_hq_5 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 0 } check_variable = { which = pe_c5_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c5_cash value >= -50 } } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 5 } } }
				if = { limit = { has_planet_flag = pe_hq_6 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 0 } check_variable = { which = pe_c6_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c6_cash value >= -50 } } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 6 } } }
				if = { limit = { has_planet_flag = pe_hq_7 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 0 } check_variable = { which = pe_c7_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c7_cash value >= -50 } } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 7 } } }
				if = { limit = { has_planet_flag = pe_hq_8 root = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 0 } check_variable = { which = pe_c8_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c8_cash value >= -50 } } } root = { set_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 8 } } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_k value > 0 } }
			set_timed_country_flag = { flag = pe_defence_hq_cd days = 360 }
			set_variable = { which = pe_df_free value = 1 }
			pe_corp_defence_threat = yes
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_df_budget value = pe_c1_cash } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_df_budget value = pe_c2_cash } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_df_budget value = pe_c3_cash } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 4 } } set_variable = { which = pe_df_budget value = pe_c4_cash } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 5 } } set_variable = { which = pe_df_budget value = pe_c5_cash } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 6 } } set_variable = { which = pe_df_budget value = pe_c6_cash } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 7 } } set_variable = { which = pe_df_budget value = pe_c7_cash } }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 8 } } set_variable = { which = pe_df_budget value = pe_c8_cash } }
			change_variable = { which = pe_df_budget value = 200 }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_power value > 0 } check_variable = { which = pe_df_budget value >= 150 } }
				pe_corp_defence_start = yes
			}
		}
		# Otherwise the strongest corporation, for a price (every two years).
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = pe_defence_cd } }
		set_variable = { which = pe_df_best value = 1499 }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c1_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c1_cash value > pe_df_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 1 } set_variable = { which = pe_df_best value = pe_c1_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c2_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c2_cash value > pe_df_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 2 } set_variable = { which = pe_df_best value = pe_c2_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c3_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c3_cash value > pe_df_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 3 } set_variable = { which = pe_df_best value = pe_c3_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c4_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c4_cash value > pe_df_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 4 } set_variable = { which = pe_df_best value = pe_c4_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c5_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c5_cash value > pe_df_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 5 } set_variable = { which = pe_df_best value = pe_c5_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c6_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c6_cash value > pe_df_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 6 } set_variable = { which = pe_df_best value = pe_c6_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c7_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c7_cash value > pe_df_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 7 } set_variable = { which = pe_df_best value = pe_c7_cash } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_c8_on value = 1 } check_variable = { which = pe_c8_cash value > pe_df_best } } set_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 8 } set_variable = { which = pe_df_best value = pe_c8_cash } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_k value > 0 } }
			set_timed_country_flag = { flag = pe_defence_cd days = 720 }
			random_owned_planet = {
				limit = { has_orbital_bombardment = yes }
				save_event_target_as = pe_df_planet
			}
			pe_corp_defence_threat = yes
			set_variable = { which = pe_df_budget value = pe_df_best }
			multiply_variable = { which = pe_df_budget value = 0.8 }
			set_variable = { which = pe_df_alloys value = pe_df_budget }
			divide_variable = { which = pe_df_alloys value = 8 }
			divide_variable = { which = pe_df_alloys value = 10 }
			round_variable = pe_df_alloys
			multiply_variable = { which = pe_df_alloys value = 10 }
			export_resource_stockpile_to_variable = { resource = alloys variable = pe_df_stock }
			set_variable = { which = pe_df_half value = pe_df_alloys }
			multiply_variable = { which = pe_df_half value = 0.5 }
			round_variable = pe_df_half
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_power value > 0 } }
				if = {
					limit = { is_ai = yes }
					if = {
						limit = { has_resource = { type = alloys amount >= 1 } }
						export_resource_stockpile_to_variable = { resource = alloys variable = pe_tmp }
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_tmp value >= pe_df_alloys } }
							add_resource = { alloys = -1 mult = pe_df_alloys }
							pe_corp_defence_start = yes
						}
					}
				}
				else = { country_event = { id = pe.294 } }
			}
		}
		}
	}
}

# Country scope: the hostile fleet power in event_target:pe_df_planet's system (pe_df_power,
# pe_df_need = 1.5 x), and the independent firms on the planet (pe_df_ind).
pe_corp_defence_threat = {
	set_variable = { which = pe_df_power value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = pe_df_ind value = 0 }
	event_target:pe_df_planet = {
		solar_system = {
			every_fleet_in_system = {
				limit = { owner = { OR = { is_hostile = root is_at_war_with = root } } }
				export_trigger_value_to_variable = { trigger = fleet_power variable = pe_tmp }
				root = { change_variable = { which = pe_df_power value = prev.pe_tmp } }
			}
		}
		every_deposit = {
			limit = { has_deposit_category = pe_deposit_cat_firms is_variable_set = pe_corp check_variable = { which = pe_corp value = 0 } }
			root = { change_variable = { which = pe_df_ind value = 1 } }
		}
	}
	set_variable = { which = pe_df_need value = pe_df_power }
	multiply_variable = { which = pe_df_need value = 1.5 }
}

# Country scope: the independent firms of event_target:pe_df_planet join the corporation pe_df_k.
pe_corp_defence_firms = {
	event_target:pe_df_planet = {
		every_deposit = {
			limit = { has_deposit_category = pe_deposit_cat_firms is_variable_set = pe_corp check_variable = { which = pe_corp value = 0 } }
			set_variable = { which = pe_corp value = root.pe_df_k }
		}
	}
}

# Country scope: the corporation pe_df_k sends its fleet to event_target:pe_df_planet.
pe_corp_defence_start = {
	set_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 1 }
	if = { limit = { has_technology = tech_destroyers } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 2 } }
	if = { limit = { has_technology = tech_cruisers } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 3 } }
	if = { limit = { has_technology = tech_battleships } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 4 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 1 } } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_ccost value = 150 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 2 } } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_ccost value = 300 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 3 } } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_ccost value = 600 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 4 } } set_variable = { which = pe_fl_ccost value = 1200 } }
	set_variable = { which = pe_df_spent value = 0 }
	create_fleet = {
		name = pe_corp_fleet_name
		settings = { uses_naval_capacity = no }
		effect = {
			set_owner = root
			set_variable = { which = pe_fp value = 0 }
			while = {
				count = 20
				limit = {
					check_variable = { which = pe_fp value < root.pe_df_need }
					root = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_spent value < pe_df_budget } }
				}
				if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 1 } } } create_ship = { name = random random_existing_design = corvette } }
				else_if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 2 } } } create_ship = { name = random random_existing_design = destroyer } }
				else_if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 3 } } } create_ship = { name = random random_existing_design = cruiser } }
				else_if = { limit = { root = { check_variable = { which = pe_fl_class value = 4 } } } create_ship = { name = random random_existing_design = battleship } }
				root = { change_variable = { which = pe_df_spent value = pe_fl_ccost } }
				export_trigger_value_to_variable = { trigger = fleet_power variable = pe_fp }
			}
			set_location = event_target:pe_df_planet
			set_fleet_stance = aggressive
			set_fleet_flag = pe_leased
			set_fleet_flag = pe_lease_defence
		}
	}
	# The leader is the holdings' own; assigned while they own and control the fleet.
	event_target:pe_hold = {
		create_leader = { class = commander species = root name = random skill = 3 }
		last_created_leader = { save_event_target_as = pe_fl_leader }
	}
	last_created_fleet = {
		set_owner = event_target:pe_hold
		assign_leader = event_target:pe_fl_leader
		set_controller = root
		add_modifier = { modifier = pe_leased_fleet days = -1 }
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 1 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c1_cash value = pe_df_spent } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c1_name } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 2 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c2_cash value = pe_df_spent } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c2_name } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 3 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c3_cash value = pe_df_spent } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c3_name } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 4 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c4_cash value = pe_df_spent } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c4_name } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 5 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c5_cash value = pe_df_spent } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c5_name } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 6 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c6_cash value = pe_df_spent } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c6_name } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 7 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c7_cash value = pe_df_spent } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c7_name } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_k value = 8 } } subtract_variable = { which = pe_c8_cash value = pe_df_spent } set_variable = { which = pe_msg_name value = pe_c8_name } }
	set_variable = { which = pe_df_q value = 12 }
	set_variable = { which = pe_df_run value = pe_df_k }
	event_target:pe_df_planet = { set_planet_flag = pe_df_target save_event_target_as = pe_msg_planet }
	if = {
		limit = { is_ai = no }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = pe_df_free value = 1 } } pe_corp_msg = { KEY = pe_msg_defence_hq } }
		else = { pe_corp_msg = { KEY = pe_msg_defence_start } }
	}
}
